# Habitify: Habit Tracker — App Store ID 1111447047 — Full Review Analysis

**App:** Habitify: Habit Tracker — *Daily Goals, Routine & Streaks*
**Developer of record in the corpus metadata:** Unstatic Ltd Co · **Bundle:** `co.vuvo.habitify` · Reviewers identify the team as Vietnamese (`4721534340`, `5810323969`, `14062845602`); one founder is addressed as "Peter" (`3356614111`, `3393688311`).
**Corpus:** 3,939 written reviews · 99 storefronts · 3 August 2016 → 2 September 2026
**Corpus mean rating:** 3.742 · **Extracted:** 8 September 2026 (`itunes MZStore userReviewsRow`)
**Analysis date:** 11 September 2026
**Business model as reviewers experience it:** free download with a **3-habit free tier** (reported as 2 at several points, including from January 2026). Aug 2016 → 16 Jan 2019: a **one-time Premium unlock** (~$3–10). From **17 Jan 2019**: monthly/annual **subscription**, with a **lifetime** option restored in April 2019. From **Oct 2020**: the free tier also caps **check-ins (15 per week)**. Cross-promotion of the developer's other apps; no third-party ads.

---

## How to read this

- Every quantified finding gives **count, percentage, denominator, scope, period, signal label and review IDs**. Every number traces back to the exact record via `review_id` in `reviews.jsonl`. **Part 11.6 lists every review ID behind every theme; Part 11.7 lists every one of the 3,939 reviews with the codes it received.**
- **Every review was read and hand-coded by the analyst**, in every language. No theme count below comes from keyword matching alone. A keyword sweep was used only afterwards, to find misses (Part 1.4).
- Theme counts are **non-exclusive**: one review can carry several themes.
- Signal thresholds: `<0.1%` ignore · `0.1–0.5%` weak · `0.5–1%` emerging · `1–3%` meaningful · `3–5%` very strong · `>5%` high-priority.
- **Facts** are what reviewers wrote. **Interpretation** is labelled as such.
- The global denominator is **3,939** unless stated. Era denominators: E1 929 · E2 1,252 · E3 530 · E4 417 · E5 811.

### ⚠️ Seven warnings before any number here is used

1. **The corpus is front-loaded.** 2018–2020 hold **2,108 reviews (53.5%)**. 2024–2026 hold 811 (20.6%), and 2022–2023 only 417 (10.6%). A global rate is therefore mostly a 2018–2020 rate. Era tables (Parts 1.6 and 8) are the corrective.
2. **One storefront's problem is not a product-wide problem.** **171 of the 173** reviews about mainland-China connectivity come from `cn`. They are 57.8% of all China reviews and 79 of its 101 1★ reviews. Excluding them, the corpus mean is **3.81** (not 3.74), and China's is **3.77** (not 2.96).
3. **Some high ratings were probably prompted.** Between 12 Oct and 1 Nov 2018, 110 reviews arrived with a **mean of 4.64 (81.8% 5★)**, against 3.30 in the six weeks before. On 12 Oct alone 32 reviews arrived (mean 4.81). The same weeks contain reviews that name the founder ("Is that okay Peter?" `3356614111`; "developed by Peter and his team" `3393688311`) and one that mentions "a strange email … asking for a rating" (`3367548037`). Separate reviewers describe a free-lifetime link sent *after* reviewing (`3897029485`) and a CEO asking for a 5★ review (`5817575296`). The 5★ share of 2018 should be read with this in mind.
4. **Short reviews inflate the positive side.** **820 reviews (20.8%)** have a body of 25 characters or fewer; their mean is 4.04 and 65.6% are 5★. **413 reviews (10.5%)** carry no theme beyond generic praise or complaint. They are counted in every denominator.
5. **No `version` field exists.** Release attribution comes only from what reviewers write (v2.0, v3.0, v5.5, v6.0, "Habitify X"/v10.0.3, v10.7.4–10.7.7, macOS 13.0.1). It is never asserted from metadata.
6. **"Payer" evidence is self-selected.** 427 reviewers (10.84%) say in the first person that they paid, subscribed or hold a lifetime licence. Payers write both when delighted (212 rate 4–5★) and when something breaks (172 rate 1–2★). They are not a sample of customers.
7. **Single-rater coding.** One analyst read and coded all 3,939 reviews against a 110-code taxonomy (Part 11.2). The keyword recall sweep corrected 33 codes (Part 11.3). Borderline calls are disclosed, but no second rater validated them.

---

# EXECUTIVE SUMMARY

**1. The product concept wins; that is the corpus's majority story.**
**51.9% of reviews are 5★ (2,044 of 3,939)** and the mean is 3.74. **Core praise appears in 1,477 reviews (37.50%, high-priority), mean 4.77.** What is praised:
- **Simplicity**, 461 (11.70%; 453 of them rated 4–5★);
- **Design**, 322 (8.17%);
- **"Best habit tracker I've tried"**, 303 (7.69%), rising from 2.9% of 2016 reviews to 10.4–10.5% of 2023–24;
- **Concrete life outcomes**, 205 (5.20%);
- **Statistics**, 187 (4.75%).

The differentiators reviewers name are specific:
- **Time-of-day sections** — morning / afternoon / evening (`2113325079`, `3294233351`, `3491889583`, `10942733003`);
- **"N times a week" and every-N-days frequency** (`1460574941`, `1470303977`, `5775951762`);
- **One account across iPhone, iPad, Watch, Mac, web, Android and Windows** (`3389713889`, `7785151407`, `14040060412`);
- **A lifetime price when rivals only rent** (`3440017816`, `7191544761`, `13449836057`).

**2. The paywall is the biggest source of friction, and it has tightened three times.**
**Monetisation friction: 521 reviews (13.23%, high-priority), mean 2.52**. Within it:
- **The 3-habit cap: 213 (5.41%), mean 2.31.** Reviewers say three habits are too few to judge the app (`3302452135`, `7834427180`, `9594953333`).
- **17 Jan 2019 — one-time Premium replaced by a subscription.** Price objection rose to **9.4% of 2019 reviews** (68). A buyer who paid £9.99 two days earlier found they had bought one year (`3619346514`). "The $10 'lifetime' option has vanished" (`3662823096`).
- **Oct 2020 — a weekly check-in limit for free users.** 29 reviews, **mean 2.00**. Long-time free users were locked out mid-streak: "After 500 days I was pay-walled" (`6541638912`), 432 days (`6515490377`), "15 log/week" (`7108556777`, `10871729516`).
- **2026 — cap complaints at 9.3% of reviews, the highest since 2016.** Several reviewers say the free tier is now two habits: "Deleted as soon as the free tier dropped to 2" (`13684177637`), `13784118583`.

**3. China was a lost market from 2018, and it was never fixed.**
**173 reviews (4.39%, very strong globally; 57.8% of China)** say that in mainland China the app cannot register, log in, save or sync without a VPN. The peak was **Dec 2018 → Jan 2019 (61 reviews)**, right after the App Store "Today" feature sent Chinese users to the app (`3528438035`, `3541075226`). They were still arriving in 2025–26:
- `12890334582`;
- `14019544061`: "you can't even log in, but the charge was fast".

Paying Chinese users could not unlock or restore what they bought (`3529813395`, `6925930580` — paid twice; `7112692402` — 328 RMB, refunds refused).

**4. Cross-device reliability is the persistent engineering weakness.**
**Platform issues — Mac/web app, Watch, widgets and sync — total 354 reviews (8.99%, high-priority), mean 2.87.** They rose from 3.7% of E1 to **18.7% of 2020–21 and 19.9% of 2022–23**.
- **Mac app stalled, 2021–22** — no sync, stuck in "Guest", "abandoned". XPLAT- reached **18.5% of 2022 reviews**. Support conceded: "the update version is only released on phone, not the MacOS yet" (`7727790054`). Fixed in macOS 13.0.1, Dec 2022 – Feb 2023 (`9359375535`, `9570248200`).
- **Watch problems — 100 reviews** (`6378964234`, `7997487916`, `9545911562`).
- **Widget problems — 85 reviews, rising to 6.3% of 2025** after the May 2025 widget redesign (`12711946464`, `12832854491`).

**5. Redesigns repeatedly removed what paying users relied on.**
**Regression — "an update made it worse" or "a feature was removed" — totals 197 reviews (5.00%), mean 2.51.** It is concentrated in five events:
- **v2.0, Nov 2016:** the charity feature was removed and data wiped (`1494883584`, `1493584000`).
- **Apr 2019:** notes and skip moved behind the paywall, and free users lost 300 days of notes (`4002480223`).
- **"Habitify X", late Aug 2020:** the timer and time-log data, yearly and daily-ratio stats, CSV export, swipe between days and "N times per week" were removed, and the Watch app broke. 149 reviews arrived in nine weeks, 42 of them UPDATE- (`6373708406`, `6372859679`, `6461110648`).
- **Notes redesign, Dec 2024 – Jan 2025:** notes can no longer be back-dated or edited (`12185327521`, `12129630081`).
- **May 2025 widget redesign** (above).

**6. Billing and entitlement integrity is smaller than at some peers, but it concentrates on the best customers.**
**147 reviews (3.73%, very strong), mean 2.22**:
- Premium not granted or lost, 69;
- refunds, 45;
- disputed charges, 42;
- cannot cancel, 13;
- promotions shown to people who already paid, 14.

Two patterns stand out:
- **The Jan 2018 purchase wave:** 21 reviews, 11 from Japan, all fixed within about two weeks (`2093117380`, `2132082291`).
- **Entitlements that don't follow the user** across a new phone, guest → account, web purchase (Paddle) → iPhone, or after an update: `6574885307`, `6934749412`, `13400575929`, `13516370858` (18 days without activation), `13732504298` (6 weeks, later resolved `14029456083`), `14023238746`.

**7. Account and data trust is the sharpest reputational risk.**
**102 reviews (2.59%), mean 1.80.**
- **Account deletion that spins forever: 47 reviews, mean 1.23, 41 of them 1★** — 2019–2025, heavily from Korea and Japan (`5202035434`, `6091415990`, `12715605250`).
- **Unwanted marketing email: 37 reviews, 27 of them in July 2023** (12.7% of all 2023 reviews). Dormant or deleted accounts received daily emails with no working unsubscribe link. Reviewers cite GDPR/ICO (`10101814356`) and CAN-SPAM (`10095812056`). A privacy professional still reported failure in May 2024 (`11244516686`).
- **Privacy: 23 reviews, mean 1.35.** They include:
  - a reviewer who saw **other customers' support conversations, emails and photos** in the in-app help (`10638299850`);
  - goal-share URLs that work without login (`8440977492`).

**8. Support is two-sided.**
**Support praised: 103 (2.61%), mean 4.73.** Support failure (unresponsive or deletion) totals **131 (3.33%), mean 1.70**. The better experiences come with quick fixes (`2096151645`, `6373930592`, `14040060412` "within one minute"). The worst involve payers:
- 5 weeks without a reply, then the review deleted (`7206302157`);
- AI-only replies (`13659075450`, `14106494461`).

**9. Payers are split, not uniformly angry.**
**427 explicit payers (10.84%), mean 3.16: 212 rate 4–5★, 172 rate 1–2★.**
- **What made them buy:**
  - the **lifetime / one-time option** — 112 payers mention it, mean 3.62;
  - **design and simplicity**;
  - **cross-platform sync**;
  - **supporting a small team** (`1504622058`, `3293870240`).
- **What goes wrong after paying:** entitlement failure (66 payers, 15.5%), reliability (182, 42.6%), support failure (51, 11.9%) and regressions (49, 11.5%).

**10. Cheapest wins, in evidence order:**
1. Make account deletion and email unsubscribe work in one tap (47 + 37 reviews, the lowest means in the corpus).
2. Make entitlements follow the Apple ID or account on every platform, and make restore work.
3. Never remove a feature that paid users rely on without a migration path.
4. Restore a free tier that lets people judge the app (e.g. 5 habits, unlimited check-ins on those habits).
5. Keep Mac, web, Watch and widgets in feature and sync parity.
6. Offer a China-reachable backend or iCloud sync.
7. Stop soliciting reviews in ways that look incentivised.

---

# PART 1 — SCOPE AND METHOD

## 1.1 Files used

| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `reviews.jsonl` | Primary corpus — 3,939 records, one JSON object per line |
| `by_country/*.jsonl` | 99 per-storefront files, used for reconciliation |
| `manifest.json` | App metadata, per-storefront counts, rating distribution |
| `_state.json` | Crawl state (not used for analysis) |

## 1.2 Schema

Every record carries: `review_id`, `app_id`, `app_name`, `country`, `country_name`, `rating`, `title`, `body`, `author`, `date` (ISO 8601 UTC), `vote_count`, `vote_sum`, `is_edited`. There are no nulls.

- **Fields used:** `review_id`, `country`, `rating`, `title`, `body`, `date`, `is_edited`, `vote_count`.
- **Fields ignored:** `author` (personal data, no analytical value); `app_id` and `app_name` (constant).
- **Votes are too sparse to weight.** `vote_count` is non-zero on 461 records. The most-voted are:
  - `3612038924` — 55 votes, 5★, Vietnam, 2019;
  - `7169126431` — 36 votes, 2★ "Money Grab", US, 2021;
  - `3314289081` — 34 votes, 5★, China, 2018;
  - `3312785510` — 26 votes;
  - `4771857078` — 25 votes, a Turkish price complaint.
- **No `version` field exists.**

## 1.3 Coverage and reconciliation

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Records in `reviews.jsonl` | **3,939** |
| Unique `review_id` values | **3,939** (zero ID duplicates) |
| Sum of the 99 `by_country/*.jsonl` line counts | **3,939** — exact match; the ID sets are identical |
| `manifest.json` → `total_reviews` | 3,939 — exact match |
| `manifest.json` → `reviews_per_country` (99 entries) | matches computed per-storefront counts **exactly, all 99** |
| `manifest.json` → `rating_distribution` | `{5:2044, 4:526, 3:383, 2:279, 1:707}` — matches **exactly** |
| `manifest.json` → `mean_rating` 3.742 | computed 3.7416 — match |
| Empty `body` / empty `title` | **0 / 0** |
| Exact `title`+`body` duplicate text | **16 groups, 50 records**, all with distinct IDs. **No records were removed**; the denominator is always 3,939. (Full list below.) |
| `is_edited = true` | 169 records, treated as normal. Several are dated update logs, e.g. `5516255548` (four dated additions, Jan–Feb 2020), `7572082701` (four successive reviews, 2021), `7656211283` (1★ → 4★ after a fix) |
| Manual-coding coverage | **3,939 of 3,939 IDs coded exactly once** (0 missing, 0 duplicated, 0 unknown IDs, 0 invalid codes) |
| Date range | 2016-08-03 → 2026-09-02 (10.1 years) |

**Duplicate-text groups.** Most are short generic reviews:
- `("good","good")` ×13;
- `("top","top")` ×4: `6329244260`, `5437264469`, `12978195444`, `12313182001`;
- `("great app","great app")` ×4;
- `("good app","good")` ×4;
- `("좋아요","좋아요")` ×3;
- nine more pairs.

**One pair is substantive and probably a double submission:** `6428238618` and `6428288301` (Switzerland, 14 Sep 2020, identical 1★ "Update is worse" texts). Both are kept, and the 2020 regression counts include both. The effect is +1 on each theme they carry.

**Every one of the 3,939 records was read in full**, in date order, in 19 batches of about 200 reviews. Nothing was sampled or keyword-selected. Every language was read and translated by the analyst: Japanese, Chinese, Korean, Russian/Ukrainian, Vietnamese, Arabic, Thai and all Latin-script languages. One-word reviews, emoji-only bodies and gibberish were read, coded (`GEN+`, `GEN0`, `JUNK`) and counted. Bodies longer than 1,500 characters were wrapped, never truncated.

## 1.4 Processing method

1. **Parse and clean.** Parse all 3,939 records programmatically; double HTML-unescape titles and bodies; collapse whitespace.
2. **Read and hand-code everything.** Emit the corpus as 19 readable batches (`id|country|rating|date|title :: body`, date order). For each batch, read every review and assign **one or more codes** from a codebook. The codebook was built inductively during batches 1–2 and extended as new themes appeared. It ends at 100 component codes plus 10 derived unions (Part 11.2). Reading notes with review IDs were kept at the head of each batch file.
3. **Retro-apply new codes.** When a code was introduced mid-corpus (e.g. `R_COUNT` in batch 4), the earlier batches were re-checked, and corrections were recorded in a patch file (Part 11.3).
4. **Verify coverage.** A script checked that every `review_id` in the corpus received codes exactly once and that every code is in the codebook. The first run found **two reviews skipped in batch 1** (`1548983585`, `1556318326`); both were then coded.
5. **Recall sweep.** 20 multilingual keyword patterns (English, Japanese, Korean, Chinese, Russian, Spanish, Portuguese, French, German, Polish, Turkish and Vietnamese forms) listed every review that matched a theme's vocabulary but did not carry that theme. **All of these candidates were read.** Most were already correct: "add" is not ADHD, "subscription" often appears in praise, "sync" is often praise. **33 corrections were made** (Part 11.3).
6. **Aggregate.** Counts, shares, means and rating profiles were computed by theme, star band, year, era, storefront and market group, plus event windows (Oct 2018 burst, Dec 2018 China wave, Jan 2019 subscription switch, Habitify X, July 2023 email wave, Sep 2025 crash).

## 1.5 Known limitations

- **One analyst, no second rater.** Borderline calls exist, e.g. whether a vague "can't use" from `cn` in 2018 is the network block (coded `CRASH` unless the network is mentioned). They are disclosed in Part 11.4 rather than resolved statistically.
- **Hand-coding removes most language bias, but not all.** Japanese, Korean and Chinese reviews are coded as thoroughly as English ones, so theme rates are comparable across storefronts. Very short non-English praise ("好", "굿", "Tốt") is coded `GEN+`/`USEFUL` and adds no theme.
- **Rating is not a feature preference.** 88 reviews carry a star rating that contradicts their text (`MISRATE`), and 82 of them are 5★ reviews that report a bug, a lost purchase or a block. Positive themes are also reported as "rated 4–5★" subsets.
- **Storefront ≠ nationality ≠ language.** 61 reviews in the Japan storefront are in Latin script, and 12 in Brazil are in Vietnamese. Country findings are storefront findings.
- **Unverifiable claims are reported as claims**, e.g. "support-chat showed other customers' conversations" (`10638299850`) and "developer deleted my review" (`7206302157`).
- **No download, revenue, retention, churn or refund-outcome data exists** in this corpus. Review volume is used only as a disclosed proxy and is never called downloads.
- **No external source was consulted** for product facts. Every feature, price and event in Parts 2–4 is attested by at least one review ID. The one external *assumption* is the membership of the "high-spend markets" group (Part 7.4).
- **3,526 of the 3,939 records (89.5%) carry at least one specific theme.** The remaining 413 (10.5%) are generic ("Good", "좋아요", "Great app"); their mean is 4.69.

The other 81 storefronts hold 749 reviews (19.0%), all included in every global number.

# PART 2 — PRODUCT AND MONETISATION MODEL

## 2.1 Feature inventory derived from reviews

Everything here is attested by review text. Nothing is taken from the store listing.

| Capability | Attested by | Free / Paid as reviewers describe it |
|---|---|---|
| Daily, specific-weekday, **"N times a week"** and (later) every-N-days frequency | `1460574941`, `1470303977`, `3295388458`, `5775951762`, `6305379316` | Free; "N times per week" was removed in Aug 2020 and later restored (`6346899859`, `6458942355`) |
| **Time-of-day sections** (morning / afternoon / evening / anytime), adjustable day start | `1534629525`, `2113325079`, `3294233351`, `5932629140` | Free |
| Reminders, including repeats every 30 min until done | `1534629525`, `1546230523`, `1821856292` | 1 reminder per habit free; **multiple reminders Premium** (`4583533909`, `6009503696`) |
| Streaks, completion rates, calendar and progress charts | `1471543599`, `2599530918`, `3440017816`, `5351195268` | Basic free; **history beyond 30 days gated in 2019** (`4207353472`) |
| **Notes / journal** per habit per day (from ~Oct 2018), natural-language dates | `3358156602`, `3527749082`, `5518407122` | **Moved to Premium Apr 2019** (`4002480223`, `4166681642`) |
| Skip and fail states | `1477618808` (2016), `5981035792` | Skip removed 2017 (`1592152210`), later returned; **Premium in 2019** (`4005130935`) |
| **Timer / stopwatch / Pomodoro** (from Oct 2019) | `4883952820`, `6211239705`, `11200846854`, `12642188195` | Free; time-log data removed in Aug 2020 (`6372859679`), timer restored 2021 (`7749321554`) |
| Goals with units and multiple check-ins per day (Aug 2020) | `6353124209`, `6358235333`, `7713495259` | Free |
| Areas / folders (Dec 2019) | `5248212573`, `6137145408` | Free |
| Habit stacking | `12041896476`, `12086710828` | Broken for long periods (`8083522711`, `12195201168`) |
| Apple Health auto-logging (≈2020–21); Fitbit, Strava, screen-time and NFC habits (2024–26) | `6496710391`, `7399087065`, `14242881753`, `13575307315`, `13291400972`, `13759922947` | Mixed: "free" (`7480042765`) vs "Premium" (`7765702076`, `14401018465`) |
| Mood log (Aug 2021) | `7665159079`, `8151088180`, `10866310493` | Multiple moods per day Premium (`9696710729`) |
| Challenges, friends and sharing (2022+) | `8982833388`, `12135068738`, `13691298674` | Some challenges gated (`8695467024`) |
| Platforms: iPhone, iPad (landscape), Apple Watch, Mac, menu-bar mini app, web, Android, Windows | `3389713889`, `4003745901`, `5332861460`, `5952039329`, `13236248497`, `14040060412` | One purchase covers all, except the 2018 Mac app, which was sold separately (`3301240499`) |
| Widgets: Today widget (2016), home-screen widgets (iOS 14, 2020), redesigned May 2025 | `1456262900`, `6544400233`, `12711946464` | Free |
| CSV export | `2093238122`, `3294255105`, `9476514584` | Removed with Habitify X (`6461110648`, `6461863220`); present again from 2022 (`8267226236`) |
| Integrations: x-callback/Shortcuts, Zapier/IFTTT, public API, Apple/Google calendar sync | `3295311733`, `7118907544`, `9183367135`, `12023309995`, `13912801657`, `11142922352` | Calendar sync described as "plus" (`12250165294`) |
| Dark mode and themes | `3301575141`, `4541759968` | **Premium in 2019–20** (`3729902277`, `5842936639`, `5874213446`) |
| Motivational-quote widget and notification copy | `1461242882`, `5865277155` | Free; the copy is often disliked (Part 3.1) |
| Passcode lock | `3301575141` | **Removed 2025** (`12506599929`) |
| Charity "points" donated per completed habit (2016) | `1465696397`, `1483150889` | **Removed in v2.0, Nov 2016** (`1494883584`) |
| AI "smart fill" and AI/chatbot support (2025–26) | `13572613236`, `13659075450`, `14106494461` | — |
| Family plan (2025) | `12840463016`, `13344142529` | Paid; sharing described as not working |

**Not present, per reviewers:**
- one-off tasks (`1461088305`, `5436679593`, `8397582957`);
- pause / vacation mode (`3285402822`, `6854783487`);
- monthly or custom-interval frequency (for most of the corpus: `1461800857`, `3601357542`, `5817575296`);
- per-habit colours and themes (`2050580709`, `6804994945`);
- an interactive widget after iOS 14 (`6544400233`, `8308869652`);
- iCloud-only sync without an account (`1934322613`, `4505011830`, `11626791974`);
- a working service in mainland China (Part 4.1);
- a monthly plan in some storefronts (`5583352286`, `11335013815`, `12163064741`).

## 2.2 Timeline of the business, as reviewers lived it

| When (from reviews) | Event | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Aug 2016 | Launch. Free tier 3 habits (some say 2 or 1); one-time Premium ~$3–4 / €4 / 25 RMB; charity points | `1461098314`, `1468380047`, `1470008848`, `1465696397` |
| **29 Nov 2016 (v2.0)** | Charity removed; history wiped for many; login now required; some Premium lost | `1494883584`, `1493584000`, `1493125287`, `1500907322`, `1495393864` |
| 2017 | Skip and notification actions removed; 14-day histogram removed; Russian UI lost; v3.0 (Sep); multiple reminders (Oct) | `1592152210`, `1516426571`, `1552349488`, `1811412629`, `1821856292` |
| Nov 2017 – Jan 2018 | Review-prompt nagging ("three in 10 seconds") | `1973100159`, `2049009103`, `2049455058` |
| **Jan 2018** | Purchase not applied — 21 reviews, 11 from Japan; fixed by ~27 Jan | `2083040453`, `2092713521`, `2093117380`, `2132082291` |
| Jun – Dec 2018 | Mainland China cannot register or log in without a VPN; worst after the Dec 2018 "Today" feature | `2756845405`, `3528438035`, `3541075226` |
| Sep 2018 | Price rise: 30 → 45 RMB; +¥500 | `3143812831`, `3230217843` |
| Oct 2018 | Mac app; 5★ burst naming "Peter"; rating-request email | `3294014241`, `3356614111`, `3367548037` |

| Jun – Sep 2019 | New app icon disliked; v6.0 | `4387717158`, `4396996014`, `4819310385`, `4401174963` |
| Oct 2019 | iOS 13 breaks notifications, Watch and Touch ID; timer launched (buggy) | `4880936953`, `4884387541`, `4923042177`, `5050548030` |
| Dec 2019 – 2020 | Areas; account deletion spins forever (23 reviews in 2020); marketing email | `5248212573`, `5202035434`, `5395539035` |

| **~27 Aug 2020 ("Habitify X", v10)** | Time logs, yearly stats, export, swipe and "N per week" removed; Watch and Shortcuts break; multi-check-in goals added | `6341543939`, `6373708406`, `6389260394`, `6353124209` |
| **Oct 2020** | **Weekly check-in cap for free users** | `6495113718`, `6515490377`, `6541638912` |
| 2021 | Mac app stops syncing; reorder breaks in v10.7.4–10.7.5; Watch fixed (Jun); mood log (Aug); timer returns; icon-rich redesign (Sep) | `7727790054`, `7656152222`, `7477852791`, `7665159079`, `7848207278` |
| 2022 | Mac app cannot sign in ("waiting for v11 macOS"); Watch complication broken since iOS 15.1 | `8212189719`, `9029892348`, `8031471211` |

| Dec 2024 – Jan 2025 | Notes redesign removes back-dating and editing | `12001725042`, `12129630081`, `12185327521` |

| Jan 2026 | "Are you sure the day is over?" pop-up on fail/skip; reorder broken; free tier reported as 2 habits | `13665455992`, `13634328613`, `13701264625`, `13684177637` |
| 2026 | Lifetime ¥19,000 in Japan; AI-only support replies | `14106494461`, `14398774902` |

## 2.3 What is gated, and the price ladder

**The gate tightened in steps:**
1. **Habit count: 3.** Reported as 2 early on (`1974587723`), in 2020–21 (`6703021557`, `7620785588`) and again from Feb 2025 – 2026 (`12267448585`, `13684177637`, `13784118583`).
2. **Features moved into Premium in 2019:** notes, skip, multiple reminders, dark mode and history beyond 30 days.
3. **Check-ins: 15 per week from Oct 2020.**
4. **Health sleep sync, checklists and some challenges** by 2025–26 (`14401018465`, `13977555592`, `8695467024`).

| Period | What an upgrade cost, per reviewers | Representative IDs |
|---|---|---|
| 2016–2018 | One-time: $3–4, €4, 25 RMB → $5–6, €6 → $9.99 / £9.99, €11, ¥1,200, 749 RUB; Sep 2018 rise to 45 RMB | `1460574941`, `1512838955`, `1468380047`, `2917668175`, `3200640926`, `3434571250`, `3528421989`, `3143812831` |
| 17 Jan – Apr 2019 | Subscription only: $9.99/mo (quoted), A$40/yr, €30/yr, £24/yr, $39.99/yr, 269 RUB | `3644199616`, `3795256803`, `3873901587`, `3887636788`, `3959201696`, `3936231580` |
| Apr 2019 – 2020 | Monthly / annual / lifetime: ¥500 / ¥3,300 / ¥4,800; ₩7,500 / – / ₩49,000–59,000; ~€7 / €27 / €44; $7.99/mo, ~$40–50 lifetime; CA$7.99 / CA$29.99 / CA$54.99; A$62; 188 RMB | `4151962952`, `4611712882`, `5348866355`, `5336471509`, `4328070287`, `5125718200`, `6186108971`, `5856846519`, `5602217006` |
| 2020–2021 | $5/mo; $25/yr; $50–90 lifetime; ₩79,000; ¥6,000–6,100; 328–400 RMB; £26.99/yr sale; 260 TL; R80/mo | `6566048336`, `7169126431`, `6715379070`, `6804994945`, `7276053699`, `7112692402`, `7036681918`, `7498411514`, `7711901965` |
| 2021–2024 | $99 lifetime (quoted); $40 lifetime = 598 RMB; $55/yr; 50% first-day offer; £1.67/mo-equivalent or £39.99 lifetime; €50 lifetime | `7957634662`, `11773675345`, `10793301666`, `10832124422`, `11614599585`, `11654766103` |
| 2025–2026 | $9/mo, $35/yr; $39.99/yr; €25/yr; $28/yr; **¥19,000 lifetime** | `12565185507`, `13583645105`, `13977135417`, `14008672142`, `14106494461`, `14398774902` |

**Interpretation, from dated price mentions:**
- The *entry* price moved from a **$3–10 one-off** (2016–18) to **$25–40 a year** or **$40–99 once**.
- The lifetime option is the model's anchor: **112 payers mention a lifetime / one-time purchase (mean 3.62)**, and reviewers cite it as the reason they chose Habitify over subscription-only rivals.
- **Regional prices are a recurring objection.** Vietnam (`4179813347`, `6343893474`), Turkey (`4771857078`, `6966124853`), Korea (`5211451534`) and Japan (`14106494461`: "unfair to base your pricing on [exchange rates] alone") all ask for local pricing.
- **A monthly plan is not offered in every storefront, or only on the web** (`5583352286` India, `11335013815`, `12163064741`, `11509675380`, `12814496096`).

## 2.4 Signals about the developer and ownership

**Responsiveness is real but uneven.**
- Praise for quick, human support: 103 reviews (`2096151645` fixed after the review; `4259261483`; `6373930592` "Hana … sent me a video"; `13306619094` "Zoey"; `14040060412` "one minute").
- A public roadmap and feature voting (`6615191705`, `7048943462`, `12492862362`).
- Developer replies are visible from 2019 onward (`3750834909`, `5746939879`, `13665455992`).

**Signals that reviewers find troubling:**
- *Review solicitation:* the Oct 2018 burst (Warning 3); "the CEO … asked me to write a 5star review meanwhile" (`5817575296`); a free-lifetime link after reviewing (`3897029485`); contests and hidden-feature games that awarded Premium (`3560594753`, `3868120875`, `4259261483`); "Who the hell is peter?" (`3879007615`).
- *Review removal:* "Now I see that the developers deleted my review!!!" (`7206302157`, single claim, unverified).
- *Cross-promotion:* ads for the developer's other apps are shown even to lifetime payers (`5469055688`, `5527838555`, `5556783335`, `11961688261`, `12158613728`). Sibling apps named: Nirow (abandoned, `3845038437`, `5473199522`) and Poly (`13136171600`).
- *Language of support:* English-only replies frustrate Japanese and Chinese users (`3618410698`, `7596083103`, `13311766586`).
- *AI support:* "Their support is AI also" (`13659075450`); "I received an instant AI response … never got [a human]" (`14106494461`).

## 3.2 The findings with the worst rating profile (highest churn risk)

Ranked by mean rating; themes with n ≥ 13.

| Theme | n | Mean | % 1★ | Why it matters |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Account deletion fails | 47 | **1.23** | 87.2% | Users cannot leave; looks like dark-pattern retention (`6091415990`: "Did you choose to block account deletion to keep customers?") |
| Privacy / data handling | 23 | 1.35 | 73.9% | GDPR / CAN-SPAM exposure; the leaked support chat (`10638299850`) |
| Unwanted marketing email | 37 | 1.41 | 81.1% | 27 in one month; drew people who had not opened the app in years back to write a 1★ |
| Cannot cancel | 13 | 1.62 | 84.6% | Web-billed (Paddle) subscriptions with no in-app cancel (`6559520547`, `8371039111`) |
| Support failure (union) | 131 | 1.70 | 67.9% | Converts every other defect into a public 1★ |
| Explicit churn | 132 | 1.83 | 52.3% | The review is the exit interview |
| Billing dispute | 42 | 1.90 | 71.4% | |
| Premium not worth it | 13 | 1.92 | 46.2% | |
| Objection to subscription | 39 | 1.95 | 51.3% | The 2019 switch |
| Weekly check-in cap | 29 | 2.00 | 51.7% | Punishes the most engaged free users |
| Crash / won't open | 94 | 2.14 | 52.1% | |
| Refund | 45 | 2.16 | 60.0% | |
| Login fails | 151 | 2.19 | 50.3% | Mostly China and the Mac app |
| Billing integrity (union) | 147 | 2.22 | 57.1% | |
| Data lost | 55 | 2.25 | 43.6% | |
| Free-tier cap | 213 | 2.31 | 40.8% | The *volume* complaint, but milder than the trust themes |
| Premium not granted | 69 | 2.33 | 52.2% | |
| China connectivity | 173 | 2.36 | 46.2% | |

**Interpretation.**
- The eight worst-rated themes are about **leaving, being contacted, being billed or being ignored**. None of them is a missing feature.
- The **free cap (2.31)** and **price objection (2.64)** are milder than the trust failures (1.23–1.95).
- As in peer apps, **people tolerate a gate; they do not tolerate being trapped, spammed or charged for nothing.**

## 3.3 Reading the monetisation objection correctly

The 521 monetisation-friction reviews make different arguments. This is a manual, overlapping, qualitative split, not additional quantified themes:

| Argument | Rough share | Example IDs |
|---|---|---|
| "Three habits is too few to *judge* the app" (evaluation) | the plurality | `1468304734` ("isn't much of a test run"), `3302452135` ("if … 5 rather than 3 … I'd have … probably upgraded"), `7834427180`, `9594953333`, `12379414402` |
| "Don't take away what I already had" (the check-in cap, features moved to Premium) | concentrated in 2019–21 | `4002480223` (300 days of notes), `4005130935`, `6495113718`, `6541638912`, `7038821896` ("limiting the number of check ins per week ruins the whole point") |
| "A tracker is not a service; don't rent it to me" (subscription principle) | 2019 spike | `3661739808` ("my habits don't need a rack of servers"), `3828231545`, `3674478475`, `3873901587`, `5336471509` |
| "Too expensive for what it is" (value) | steady | `3936231580` ("269 roubles … I pay 400 for Netflix"), `5884634297` ("43 euro … for an agenda???"), `6186108971`, `7169126431`, `12565185507` |
| "Price it for my country / my income" (affordability) | steady, non-US | `4179813347` (Vietnam), `4771857078` (Turkey, 270 TL), `5071251596` (high-school student), `6257885145` (student, deleted), `13153252441` ("After 2 years … I finally can afford"), `14106494461` (¥19,000) |
| "Let me pay monthly / try it first" (commitment) | E5 | `5583352286`, `10866310493`, `11363702706`, `12814496096` ("pagar 50-60€ como única opción?") |

**Against these sit 99 price-praise reviews (mean 4.85) and 51 one-time/lifetime praises (mean 4.63).** Examples:
- "the one time cost of $9.99 for lifetime use … Most habit apps look to charge a monthly subscription... Habitify does not" (`3440017816`);
- "Cheap life time subscription!" (`6058800143`);
- "permanent premium is only equal to other apps' annual fee" (`6481448783`);
- "I would have paid triple" (`14234671252`).

**Interpretation:** the cap itself is tolerated by many, including 18 who defend it (`2524381507`, `5049998510`, `14107414859`). What turns a free user into a 1★ is:
- losing access they already had (notes, check-ins);
- a price set without regard to local income;
- having no way to try Premium before a year-long commitment.

**Broken or regressed — the capability existed and stopped working (Part 4):** China access, entitlement, Mac/web sync and sign-in, Watch, widgets, notes, the timer, export, streak display, "N per week", ordering, Health counts, account deletion, email opt-out.

**Misunderstandings (a UX problem, not a feature gap)** — complexity is 116 reviews and rises to 5.8% of E5:
- the time-of-day filter hides habits (`5224462090`: "I had previously said that the app is buggy … it was because of the selected time of day filter");
- how to reorder (`6864103347`) and how to delete a habit (`5938727222`);
- skip vs cancel vs fail (`12610965791`);
- "every day starts skipped" (`12785440572`, a setting);
- where the timer is (`7521935821`, `9771138270`);
- how to connect the Watch (`14135755783`).

## 3.6 Competitors named in the corpus

**217 reviews (5.51%) name a competitor or say they "tried many" — mean 4.31, 152 of them 5★.** Unlike peers in decline, **Habitify is mostly the destination**:
- **Arrivals.** From Productive (`2077625655`, `3440017816`, `4902293402`), Way of Life (`2052461760`), HabitBull (`4100540802`, `5421125900`), Strides (`5932629140`), Momentum (`4733753637`), Done and Habit (`5436429394`), Notion (`9963220481`), HabitMinder (`7749321554`, returned), Streaks (`14040060412`).
- **Departures / threats.** Streaks — one-time price, iCloud sync, better Health (`3619346514`, `4004922568`, `7997487916` "a tenth of the price", `11264323131`); Everyday (`9545911562`); Fabulous (`8330729266`); TickTick (`6837148923`, `12871409625`); Loop, free (`13779571391`); Awesome Habits (`13606005182`); Atoms (`11598703636`); Way of Life (`8215055300`).
- **Complements:** Things (`1811412629`), Structured (`8482240585`), Todoist (`6565634510`), Habitica (`10113019619`).

---

# PART 4 — THE SIX CLUSTERS THAT EXPLAIN THE NEGATIVE SIDE

## 4.1 Cluster 1 — Mainland China could not use the app `[very strong globally; 57.8% of China]`

**173 reviews (4.39%), mean 2.36; 171 of them from the `cn` storefront.** By era they are 11.5% of E1 and fall to 1.2% of E5. That fall reflects fewer Chinese reviews, not a fix: `cn` volume dropped from 162 in E1 to 12 in E5.

| Window | Symptom (as reviewers describe it) | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Oct 2016 – 2017 | Mainland download shows the Japanese UI and cannot register; login fails after an update | `1469161177`, `1493380781`, `1504505526`, `1512751825` ("only via VPN can I log in") |
| Jun & Sep 2018 | Stuck on the tour spinner; "Oh no! Something went wrong" at registration; saved habits never appear | `2752887130` ("should tell users at the start that the app needs a VPN"), `2756845405` ("the Great Firewall … mandatory registration"), `3161727412`, `3188961175` |
| **13 Dec 2018 – 10 Jan 2019** | **Same failure after an App Store "Today" feature: 68 China reviews, mean 2.34** | `3528438035` ("can't register — and you recommended it on the home page?"), `3541075226` (downloaded from Today), `3589796422`, `3590376404`, `3617585831` |
| 2019 – 2021 | Sync and login need a VPN; paid users cannot use what they bought | `3743506421` (iPhone/iPad/Mac only sync on VPN), `4736772525` (subscribed; "must use VPN in China"), `5602217006` ("I'd pay 188 RMB lifetime if you fixed the server"), `6925930580`, `7112692402` (328 RMB lifetime, refunds refused) |
| 2022 – 2026 | Still unresolved: "10 minutes to open", login spinner, "can't log in in China recently" | `8227319958` ("thank you for the Chinese localisation … but I simply cannot log in"), `8757334591`, `12890334582`, `13572613236` ("I want to pay but doubt it works in China"), `14019544061` |

**Impact:**
- **China's mean is 2.96**, but **3.77 without these reviews**. 79 of China's 101 1★ reviews are this one problem.
- 18 explicit payers are affected. 17 China reviews give 5★ while describing the block (`3184955956`, `3202158097`, `3589796422`), so the problem is under-represented in the rating itself.

**Interpretation.**
- Reviewers who explain the architecture describe mandatory sign-in to a non-Apple backend. Later reviews name Firebase (`11626791974`, `12000656068`), and several ask for plain iCloud sync (`3208341657`, `3555416943`, `5716375250`).
- China reviewers repeatedly praise the design and the localisation, and several were ready to pay. The market was not lost on product fit; it was lost on reachability.

## 4.2 Cluster 2 — The paywall tightened in steps `[high-priority as a union]`

**Monetisation friction: 521 (13.23%), mean 2.52.** The narrower **free-tier union (cap, check-in cap, general paywall) is 304 (7.72%), mean 2.32, with 131 of them 1★.**

| Step | When | What reviewers report | n | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| (a) The habit cap | Always | 3 habits (sometimes 2) on the free tier | 213 (5.41%) | `1461098314` ("at that point just sell it for 3.99"), `3463341082` ("you finally mention that habits are limited … after they've created it"), `5615780125`, `12730282337` |
| (b) Subscription replaces one-time | 17 Jan 2019 | "The $10 lifetime option has vanished"; a recent buyer is converted to one year; "my habits don't need a rack of servers" | subscription objection 39 (29 in 2019); price objection 9.4% of 2019 | `3619346514`, `3662823096`, `3661739808`, `3674478475`, `3879350608` ("from an $8 one-time payment to $40 a year") |
| (c) Features move into Premium | Apr 2019 | Notes (with 300 days of history), skip, multiple reminders, dark mode, history beyond 30 days | part of paywall 89 + removed 82 | `4002480223`, `4005130935`, `4010084040`, `4207353472`, `5842936639` |
| (d) Weekly check-in cap | Oct 2020 → 2024 | Free users can tick only ~15 check-ins a week; long streaks locked | 29 (0.74%); 14 in 2020, 8 in 2021, 6 in 2024; **mean 2.00** | `6495113718` (2 years of data), `6515490377` (432 days), `6541638912` (500 days), `7038821896`, `7108556777`, `11354879589` ("three habits is reasonable. The problem … I can't check into those habits more than 15 times a week") |
| (e) Upsell surfaces | 2023 → 2026 | Full-screen offer on every open; a banner covering the third habit; a banner that cannot be closed; notices after paying | upsell 22 (1.7% of E5) + ads to payers 14 | `12730282337`, `13022842033` ("I have adhd … huge banner … you CAN NOT CLOSE"), `13061919171`, `13610321423`, `13688287990`, `10620666635` (premium user: "No. deal with it") |
| (f) Free tier cut | Feb 2025 → 2026 | Two habits; "free is just a demo" | cap is 9.3% of 2026 (20 reviews since Dec 2025) | `12267448585`, `13684177637`, `13784118583`, `14057584186` ("slowly chipping away at the most basic features") |
| (g) No trial, then auto-converting trials | throughout; 2025–26 | "no chance to try"; a trial silently converts to an annual charge | trial 31 (mean 2.19) | `7032422476`, `11363702706`, `13422214812`, `13550132289`, `14019544061` |

**What the evidence says, and does not say.**
- The cap alone is not the whole story: 18 reviewers defend it and 48 call the free tier sufficient.
- The spikes in anger follow the **take-backs** — steps (b), (c), (d) and (f) — rather than the cap.
- The corpus cannot measure conversion. It can show that each tightening produced its own wave of 1★ reviews from engaged free users.

## 4.3 Cluster 3 — Billing and entitlement integrity `[very strong]`

**147 reviews (3.73%), mean 2.22, 84 of them 1★.**
- **Premium not granted, lost or not restorable: 69 (1.75%), mean 2.33.**
- **Refunds: 45.**
- **Billing disputes: 42.**
- **Cancellation traps: 13.**
- **Ads or upsell shown to payers: 14.**
- **Discount not honoured: 3.**

| Mechanism | n (approx.) | Window | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Store purchase not applied** | 21 in Jan 2018 (11 Japan); a trickle later | Jan 2018 wave, fixed ~27 Jan 2018; recurring 2019–26 | `2077043653`, `2083040453`, `2092713521`, `2093117380`, `2096241882`; fixed `2132082291`, `2096151645` ("thank you for responding right after my review"). Later: `3730665503` (paid monthly three times), `5058976402`, `10292077249`, `10693981551`, `12270453636`, `13951351680` |
| **Entitlement doesn't follow the person** — new phone, iPad ↔ iPhone, Mac ↔ iOS, guest → account, web (Paddle) → iPhone | ~25 | 2018 → 2026 | `2098694832`, `3565627936`, `4502731816`, `6574885307` (stolen phone), `6811003189` (guest → Apple ID), `6925930580` (lifetime tied to one email; paid twice), `7107343222`, `7272591152`, `13400575929` (Paddle receipt), `13516370858` (18 days), `13732504298` → `14029456083` (resolved after 6+ weeks, 20+ emails) |
| **Update or redesign removes Premium** | ~6 | 2016, 2019, 2020, 2026 | `1495393864` (v2.0 "wiped out my premium status"), `5333682703`, `6402220006` ("habits and progress … as well as my subscription"), `14023238746` (lifetime asked to subscribe on the Watch) |
| **Charged more than shown** | ~6 | 2019 → 2020 | `5155111757` (¥4,900 shown, ¥5,150 charged), `6412142626` ($39.99 offer, $48.97 charged), `6677079669` (monthly chosen, annual charged), `10513138749` (lifetime, then an annual charge) |
| **Cannot cancel / charged after cancel or deletion** | 13 | 2019 → 2026 | `6559520547` ("payments … through a third party and there's no 'unsubscribe' button"), `8371039111` (no cancel option; charged >1,700 RUB), `7363611676` (deleted account, still charged), `13684263840`, `13550132289` |
| **Refund routed to Apple, then refused** | part of 45 | 2019 → 2026 | `7297383865` ("they're unable to process refunds"), `6461110648` ("denied a refund due to the company's arrangement with Apple"), `10201858110` (a child's 598 RMB, refused twice), `11917816355`, `14165773730` |
| **Ads for the developer's other apps shown to payers** | 14 | 2018 → 2025 | `2082489237`, `2089193559` (Nirow ad blocks use), `5556783335` ("False advertisement – Still get ads with lifetime premium"), `5652896336`, `12158613728` |

**Interpretation.**
- Unlike some peers, Habitify's integrity problems are **not** a sustained wave. The largest single event (Jan 2018) was fixed within about two weeks.
- The durable defect is **entitlement portability across the platforms that are Habitify's selling point.** A web purchase that does not unlock the iPhone, or a lifetime tied to one e-mail, turns the cross-platform promise into a support case.

## 4.4 Cluster 4 — Cross-device reliability `[high-priority as a union]`

**Platform issues union: 354 (8.99%), mean 2.87.**
- **Mac / web / iPad / Android: 141** — 18.5% of 2022 reviews.
- **Sync: 119.**
- **Watch: 100.**
- **Widgets: 85.**
- Login failures outside China cluster with the Mac app (`LOGIN` is 13.7% of 2022).

| Surface | Timeline | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| **Mac app** | 2018 bought separately and buggy → 2020 unavailable in the RU/KR stores → 2021 no sync with iOS → 2022 cannot sign in, stuck as "Guest", "abandoned" → **fixed in macOS 13.0.1** (Dec 2022 – Feb 2023) → 2025 slow (1 min to open) → Oct 2025 sync broken again | `3166206833`, `5974877379`, `5975638358`, `7727790054` (support: "the update version is only released on phone, not the MacOS yet"), `8387555235`, `9029892348` ("left for a year at a time"), `9250877212`, `9359375535`, `9570248200`, `13503164488`, `13332457934` |
| **Web app** | Slow; duplicate habits; fewer features | `5494522026` ("1 minute to reach the site and tick the first habit"), `6295806726`, `8301263103` |
| **Apple Watch** | iOS 13 (Oct 2019) → **won't launch after Habitify X** (Aug–Oct 2020) → fixed Jun 2021 → complication broken since iOS 15.1 (Oct 2021 – 2022) → sync lag → "finally" fixed Jan 2024 → sync issues 2024–26; area complications and single-tap logging removed | `4880936953`, `6378964234`, `6385690908`, `6462372951`, `7477852791`, `7885792414`, `7997487916` ("Streaks costs a tenth and works"), `9545911562`, `10842104134`, `11020040946`, `13224394178`, `12081627373` |
| **Widgets** | 2016 Today widget removed ("the entire reason I paid", `1456032628`) → iOS 14 widgets non-interactive (2020) → not refreshing on iPad for years → **May 2025 redesign** removes compact, incremental and streak widgets | `6544400233`, `8308869652` (bought for the old widget), `6798110337`, `12402275998`, `12703753311`, `12711946464`, `12832854491`, `13448603169`, `13981272932` |
| **Sync & sign-in** | Account-based sync; personal iCloud sync removed; Firebase-only; repeated logouts | `9382891858`, `9491548292` ("Original Habitify was able to sync … over personal iCloud. This has been removed"), `11626791974`, `12000656068`, `7907020932` (sign in daily), `14165773730` |

**Interpretation.**
- Cross-platform availability is a top reason people choose and pay for Habitify (106 praise reviews rated 4–5★).
- It is also the top engineering complaint of 2020–23.
- The pattern is **one platform shipping ahead of the others** (iOS first, Mac months later), so users who bought for parity experience broken parity.

## 4.5 Cluster 5 — Redesigns removed what users relied on `[very strong]`

**Regression union: 197 (5.00%), mean 2.51 — 14.9% of E3.** "An update made it worse": 170. "A feature was removed": 82.

| Event | When | What was lost | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| v2.0 | 29 Nov 2016 | Charity points; data and Premium wiped; login forced | `1494883584`, `1493584000` (30 routines gone), `1499882108` ("it's mainly what motivated me"), `1500907322` |
| v3 era | 2017 | Skip, notification actions, 14-day histogram, Russian UI, morning/day/evening sections | `1592152210`, `1516426571`, `1552349488`, `1556286849` |
| Paywall move | Apr 2019 | Notes and skip for free users; percentage stats; yearly totals | `4002480223`, `4005130935`, `3892831632`, `3896982142` |
| **"Habitify X" (v10)** | **~27 Aug 2020** | **Time log and timer data; daily ratio and yearly stats; month swipe; visible date bar; "N per week"; CSV export; Siri Shortcuts; non-daily streaks; Watch app** | **Window 27 Aug – 31 Oct 2020: 149 reviews, mean 3.14, 42 UPDATE-, 22 REMOVED.** `6341543939` ("Version X … breaks habits … no migration path"), `6372859679` (4 months of time logs vanished), `6373708406` ("I paid for the lifetime subscription because the old app was exactly what I wanted … $40 down the drain"), `6384710442`, `6389260394`, `6390610879` (weekday habits now break streaks), `6461110648` |
| Fire-streak and journal changes | 2021 – 2022 | Streak count on the complete button; "fire" streak visual | `7779015433`, `7871024411`, `8292117837` |
| Notes redesign | Dec 2024 – 2026 | Back-dating and editing notes; custom dates | `12001725042`, `12125726713`, `12129630081`, `12185327521` (1,000+ gym days logged in memos), `13991900844` |
| Widget redesign | May 2025 | Compact, incremental (+1) and streak widgets | `12703753311`, `12711946464`, `12832854491` |
| Passcode lock | 2025 | App passcode | `12506599929` |
| Fail / skip friction | Jan 2026 | "Are you sure the day is over?" pop-up with no off switch | `13634328613`, `13665455992` ("infantilizing … Shipping annoying stuff without an off switch is a churn risk") |

**The same releases also drew praise.**
- Habitify X added multi-check-in goals, which answered the largest 2017–19 request (`6353124209`: "The latest update appears to have mostly fixed my two previous concerns"; `6358235333`; `6413379285`).
- Later updates earned "Great update" and "Vastly improved" (`7848207278`, `8215877352`, `9425929290`).
- **Interpretation:** the problem is **removal without migration or a toggle**. The long-tenure users who notice removals are disproportionately lifetime payers (Part 6.7).

## 4.6 Cluster 6 — Account, email and data trust `[meaningful; sharpest ratings]`

**102 reviews (2.59%), mean 1.80, 67 of them 1★.** The share is 7.9% of E4 because of the 2023 email wave.

**(a) Account deletion that never completes — 47 (1.19%), mean 1.23, 41 of them 1★.**
- By year: 2019 2 · **2020 23** · 2021 7 · 2022 2 · 2023 10 · 2024 1 · 2025 2. Korea accounts for 16 (5.5% of Korea).
- Evidence: `5006290727` (wanted to switch apps; support silent; "emails keep coming, so I can't uninstall"), `5202035434`, `5436553027`, `5683409156`, `5875469166`, `6091415990` ("I trust it isn't deliberate… but inflating users this way looks bad"), `6434454672`, `7333094952` (crash on delete), `12715605250` ("once you ask the chat bot … it stops answering"), `12515577285`.

**(b) Unwanted marketing email — 37 (0.94%), mean 1.41; 27 in 2023 (12.7% of that year).**
- **The July 2023 wave (28 Jun – 31 Aug: 59 reviews, mean 2.88; 9 reviews on 5 July alone, mean 1.67).** Reviewers who had not opened the app in one to two years, or had deleted their account, suddenly received daily "habit" emails with no working unsubscribe link:
  - `10096299644`: "They probably do this on purpose to inflate their active user stats";
  - `10101814356`: "daily spam messages that contain all my data … I'll report them to the ICO for breaching GDPR";
  - `10095812056`: CAN-SPAM;
  - also `10084398148`, `10105286283`, `10106326416`.
- **Response:** unsubscribe was added quickly (`10109604865`: "they, quite quickly fixed it"; `10125754353`; `10206423262` raised to 3★).
- **Residue:** `10297034393` (Aug 2023) and `11244516686` (May 2024, a privacy professional: "The Unsubscribe button … doesn't work … this is simply embarassing").

**(c) Privacy — 23 (0.58%), mean 1.35.** Single-reviewer claims, reported as claims:
- the **in-app help "messages" list showed conversations between support and other customers, including emails and photos** (`10638299850`, Nov 2023);
- **goal-share URLs readable without login**, with no way to turn sharing off (`8440977492`);
- sync requiring an Apple ID, a Google account *and* an email (`9231182178`);
- a location permission (`5348615613`);
- third-party analytics in the privacy policy (`6321445666`, `12284289977`);
- forced login since 2016 (`1500907322`, `2913632619`, `3837963170`).

**(d) Guest-account confusion — 26.** Users who never created an account lose everything on update, reinstall or Mac sign-in: `4188962054` (support merged the data), `5259328224` (told to start again), `6938277719` ("Never use guest mode"), `7261000773` (a ¥6,000 lifetime bought as a guest).

**Interpretation.**
- This cluster is small in volume but carries **the lowest ratings in the corpus**.
- It is also the only one with **regulatory exposure**: data-subject deletion, email consent, and the confidentiality of support conversations.
- It is cheap to fix relative to its reputational cost.

# PART 5 — RATINGS ANALYSIS

The corpus is 5★-dominant (51.9%) with a real 1★ tail (18.0%). Each band is a different population. Shares below are of that band.

## 5.1 5★ — n = 2,044 (51.89% of corpus)

| Theme | n | % of 5★ |
|---|---|---|
| Core praise (union) | 1,240 | 60.7% |
| Simplicity | 392 | 19.2% |
| Generic only | 347 | 17.0% |
| Helps build habits | 292 | 14.3% |
| "Best tracker" | 276 | 13.5% |
| Design | 230 | 11.3% |
| Any request (unmet) | 188 | 9.2% |
| Life outcome | 186 | 9.1% |
| Reliability complaint | 162 | 7.9% |
| Explicit payer | 159 | 7.8% |
| Statistics | 155 | 7.6% |
| Competitor comparison | 152 | 7.4% |
| Monetisation friction | 86 | 4.2% |
| Rating contradicts text | 82 | 4.0% |

**Four sub-populations inside 5★:**
- **Short affective reviews** (17.0% generic only), e.g. `1456186344` "Best".
- **Comparison essays** that choose Habitify over named rivals (`3304559331`, `3491889583`, `5440406266`, `13644168457`).
- **Outcome stories** (`4823889316`, `5065106375`, `11951396915`).
- **Complaints filed at 5★ — 82 reviews.** Examples: `3184955956` (China block), `5974967423` (data lost), `6402220006` ("Progress Gone!!! Subscription cancelled"), `12251927719` (can't open for a week). Some probably rate 5 to be noticed; the rest are mis-taps.
- The Oct 2018 burst (Warning 3) also sits here.

## 5.2 4★ — n = 526 (13.35%)

| Theme | n | % of 4★ |
|---|---|---|
| **Any request (unmet)** | **184** | **35.0%** |
| Core praise | 178 | 33.8% |
| Reliability | 153 | 29.1% |
| Monetisation friction | 68 | 12.9% |
| Simplicity | 61 | 11.6% |
| Bug | 59 | 11.2% |
| Platform issues | 56 | 10.6% |
| Design | 55 | 10.5% |
| Scheduling request | 37 | 7.0% |
| Cap | 31 | 5.9% |
| Units / icons | 26 | 4.9% |
| Themes / colours | 25 | 4.8% |

**4★ is the feature-request band.** Requests are 35.0% of 4★ against 13.46% globally. Reviewers name the missing piece between 4 and 5:
- "if these options are one day added, I will update my rating to 5 stars" (`3092330445`);
- weekly completion rates and negative habits (`5462712336`);
- a habit score (`7386841861`);
- interactive widgets and Mac parity (`12757003557`).

## 5.3 3★ — n = 383 (9.72%)

| Theme | n | % of 3★ |
|---|---|---|
| **Reliability** | **183** | **47.8%** |
| Any request | 94 | 24.5% |
| Platform issues | 82 | 21.4% |
| Monetisation friction | 81 | 21.1% |
| Bug | 56 | 14.6% |
| Price objection | 41 | 10.7% |
| Cap | 34 | 8.9% |
| Mac / web | 28 | 7.3% |
| Widget | 28 | 7.3% |
| Watch | 26 | 6.8% |

**3★ is the "good app, broken on my other device" band:** `5441810150`, `6144410650`, `6390933328`, `12815466155`, `13236248497`, `13572292515`.

## 5.4 2★ — n = 279 (7.08%)

| Theme | n | % of 2★ |
|---|---|---|
| Reliability | 135 | 48.4% |
| Monetisation friction | 82 | 29.4% |
| Bug | 51 | 18.3% |
| Cap | 42 | 15.1% |
| **Regression** | **42** | **15.1%** |
| Explicit payer | 42 | 15.1% |
| Update made it worse | 38 | 13.6% |
| Price objection | 35 | 12.5% |
| **Explicit churn** | **34** | **12.2%** |
| China connectivity | 24 | 8.6% |

**2★ is the "I used to love it" band:**
- Regression is 15.1% here vs 5.0% globally, and churn is 12.2% vs 3.4%.
- Examples: `6373708406`, `6384710442`, `6486868709`, `6546552236`, `7321970137` ("almost 2 years … finally decided it's time to move on"), `13235819413`.

## 5.5 1★ — n = 707 (17.95%)

| Theme | n | % of 1★ |
|---|---|---|
| **Reliability** | **317** | **44.8%** |
| **Monetisation friction** | **204** | **28.9%** |
| **Explicit payer** | **130** | **18.4%** |
| Platform issues | 103 | 14.6% |
| Support failure | 89 | 12.6% |
| Cap | 87 | 12.3% |
| Billing integrity | 84 | 11.9% |
| **China connectivity** | **80** | **11.3%** |
| Login fails | 76 | 10.7% |
| Regression | 71 | 10.0% |
| Explicit churn | 69 | 9.8% |
| Account / email / privacy | 67 | 9.5% |
| Account deletion | 41 | 5.8% |
| Marketing email | 30 | 4.2% |

**What drives the 1★ band:**
- **Money gates** (28.9%) and **reliability** (44.8%) dominate.
- **Nearly one 1★ in five comes from a payer.**
- **China alone is 11.3% of all 1★.**
- **Trust failures** (deletion, email, privacy) are 9.5% of 1★ but only 2.6% of the corpus. They are the most over-represented group in the band.
- The register is sharp in several languages: 「詐欺アプリ」 *"scam app"* (`5058976402`, `5516255548`), "Abzocke" *"rip-off"* (`3873901587`, `5731725220`), "CON ARTISTS" (`6559520547`), 流氓 *"rogue"* (`3830846002`).

## 5.6 Themes that cut across the rating line

| Theme | 5★+4★ | 3★ | 2★+1★ | Reading |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Free cap | 50 | 34 | 129 | Mostly hostile; a quarter comes from people rating 4–5★ |
| Price objection | 59 | 41 | 103 | Many "great app, too expensive" 4–5★ |
| Update made it worse | 43 | 25 | 102 | Loyal users object at 4–5★ before leaving |
| Mac / web broken | 47 | 28 | 66 | Tolerated by fans; decisive for multi-device buyers |
| Watch broken | 34 | 26 | 40 | Evenly split |
| Widget broken | 34 | 28 | 23 | **Mildest complaint (mean 3.15)** — annoying, rarely fatal |
| China connectivity | 45 | 24 | 104 | 17 China reviewers rate 5★ while blocked |
| Explicit payer | 212 | 43 | 172 | Payers are the most polarised group |
| Competitor named | 182 | 6 | 29 | Overwhelmingly arrivals |

**The single most important cross-cutting fact:**
- The praised qualities (simplicity, design, cross-platform, lifetime) are the same qualities cited in the 2★/1★ reviews as *what was lost*.
- The product concept is not what fails. **Parity, portability and take-backs** are.

# PART 6 — PAID-USER AND UPGRADE ANALYSIS

## 6.1 Framing this correctly

| Group | Definition | n | % of corpus | Mean |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Explicit payers** | First-person "I paid / bought / subscribed / upgraded", "lifetime member", "premium user", in any language | **427** | 10.84% | **3.16** |
| …mentioning a lifetime / one-time purchase | Payer review that names lifetime, one-time, 買い切り, 永久, 평생, 终身, пожизненн… | 112 | 2.84% | 3.62 |
| Global | All reviews | 3,939 | 100% | 3.74 |

**No conversion rate is claimed, and none can be claimed from review text.**

## 6.2 Payers are polarised, and their mood tracks integrity events, not price

| Era | Explicit payers | % of era | Mean |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 (2016 – Jan 2019) | 92 | 9.9% | 2.82 |
| E2 (Jan 2019 – Aug 2020) | 126 | 10.1% | **3.77** |
| E3 (Aug 2020 – 2021) | 79 | **14.9%** | **2.78** |
| E4 (2022–23) | 47 | 11.3% | 2.96 |
| E5 (2024–26) | 83 | 10.2% | 3.10 |

- **2018 is the low point among payers (54 payers, mean 2.83): 25 of them report a purchase not applied** — the Jan 2018 wave and v2.0 losses.
- **E3 has the highest payer share (14.9%) and a mean of 2.78.** These are lifetime holders reacting to Habitify X, the check-in cap and the Mac stall.
- **E2, when a lifetime option coexisted with a stable app, is the payer high (3.77).**
- **Interpretation:** payers turn hostile when what they bought changes, not when the price rises.

## 6.3 What made people buy

| Trigger | Evidence |
|---|---|
| **A lifetime / one-time price instead of rent** | `2917668175` ("the price was subscription based … Habitify allows you to unlock all premium features with a 1 time in-app purchase of $10"), `3440017816`, `3613334476` ("not willing to go the subscription way"), `5194210441` ("you'll pay double in a year for some other similar apps"), `7191544761`, `13449836057` |
| Design and simplicity | `1470008848` (bought within the week, "really worth it"), `1789612595` ("willingly bought membership"), `5518407122` (bought lifetime "attracted by the clean design"), `6358235333` |
| Statistics | `3158042031` ("The statistics … is what sold me"), `5770923833` ("because it shows stats … I bought premium") |
| **Cross-platform access** (Mac, web, Watch) | `3678466422` ("since I get Mac OS, iPad, and Apple Watch apps all for one subscription … I'm fine with it"), `5193130183`, `6328737124` ("signed up for premium mainly because this has a web interface"), `6341023589` |
| More than three habits | `2008162858`, `4509262890`, `9491502032` |
| Supporting a small team | `1498848084`, `1504622058`, `1546230523`, `3293870240` ("I can see this team cares") |
| Specific paid features: multiple reminders, timer analytics, mood, ADHD support | `6309480498`, `10038284177` ("With the premium subscription, I can add multiple reminders"), `9696710729` |
| Sales, first-day discounts, contests | `10832124422` (50% on day one), `12881867268` (lifetime on sale "on a whim … well worth the money"), `4259261483` (won lifetime in a Twitter contest) |
| A free month, then monthly, then annual (ad-acquired) | `6277375380` — the one fully described funnel in the corpus |

## 6.4 What buyers value once they have paid

Among payers, the leading praise codes are:
- **price fair / worth it: 41 (9.6%, mean 4.85)**;
- simplicity 30;
- design 28;
- "best tracker" 25;
- life outcome 24;
- one-time pricing 13;
- support praise 15.

Lifetime holders use the language of investment: "the best £30 I ever spent" (`5065106375`); "some of the best money I ever spent on an app" (`13421769372`); "I would have paid triple" (`14234671252`).

## 6.5 What goes wrong after payment — ranked by severity

| Problem | n (within 427 payers) | % of payers | Mean | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Any reliability issue | 182 | 42.6% | 2.37 | Part 4.4 |
| **Billing & entitlement integrity** | **114** | **26.7%** | **2.23** | Part 4.3 |
| …Premium not granted / lost | 66 | 15.5% | 2.30 | `2092713521`, `6934749412`, `13732504298` |
| Support failure | 51 | 11.9% | 2.02 | `2105200122` (three refund requests unanswered), `7206302157`, `14165773730` |
| Regression after paying | 49 | 11.5% | 2.24 | `6373708406`, `6682256233`, `12711946464` |
| Refund requested | 33 | 7.7% | 2.09 | `2093117380`, `6414440114`, `12154261985` |
| Sync failure | 28 | 6.6% | 2.89 | `8605522610`, `8947613685` |
| **Explicit churn** | **27** | 6.3% | **1.78** | `6359290822` ("Lost a premium customer"), `6428238618` |
| Data lost | 25 | 5.9% | 2.40 | `1497758838`, `5887612478` (two years, twice) |
| Ads / upsell after paying | 13 | 3.0% | 2.54 | `5556783335`, `10620666635`, `12606209462` ("I wish the upgrade button would disappear when you buy premium") |
| Premium not worth it | 9 | 2.1% | 1.78 | `8330729266`, `13449001668` |

- **These are segment rates** on a 427-review denominator, not global rates. The global equivalents are entitlement failure 1.75% and support failure 3.33%.
- **The 172 payers who rate 1–2★** most often cite:
  - entitlement (42);
  - unresponsive support (38);
  - bugs (30);
  - an update made it worse (24);
  - refund (24);
  - churn (22);
  - billing dispute (18);
  - data loss (16).

## 6.6 Trial reaction is negative both ways

**Trial is discussed in 31 reviews (0.79%), mean 2.19, 15 of them 1★.**
- **For most of the corpus there was no trial**, and people say so:
  - "Three habits, and three days, is simply too little" (`7032422476`);
  - "No free trial" (`8165243761`);
  - "I wasn't given the option for a premium trial period" (`10866310493`).
- **When trials appeared (2025–26), reviewers say they converted to an annual charge unexpectedly:**
  - "I signed up for a free trial and somehow got converted to paid" (`13422214812`);
  - an annual charge after a 7-day trial, then cancelling the renewal ended current access (`13550132289`);
  - "it said a 7- or 14-day trial, then charged a full year of Pro" (`14019544061`, China).

## 6.7 The lifetime holder is the most sensitive customer

**Interpretation.** A lifetime purchase is a bet on continuity. The reviews where lifetime holders turn hostile are about removals, not price:
- "I paid for the lifetime subscription because the old app was exactly what I wanted. It never occurred to me that they would update it and actually remove things … I wish I had got the monthly subscription so I could cancel" (`6373708406`);
- 9 months in, history navigation broken (`6682256233`);
- widgets ruined after the May 2025 update (`12711946464`);
- the passcode lock removed (`12506599929`);
- reordering never works (`13701264625`);
- "a lifetime subscriber … on the Watch it keeps asking me to subscribe" (`14023238746`).

A lifetime model obliges the developer to migrate, not delete.

# PART 7 — COUNTRY AND MARKET ANALYSIS

## 7.1 Eligibility

Storefronts with **≥50 reviews** get individual analysis: **18 storefronts, 3,190 of 3,939 reviews (81.0%)**. The other 81 storefronts (749 reviews, 19.0%) are included in every global number; standalone claims about them are labelled `[limited evidence]`. Themes were hand-coded in every language, so theme rates **are comparable across storefronts**. Storefronts under 100 reviews still have limited depth.

**Era mix matters for comparing storefronts.** China is 55% E1 (162 of 296), Korea 64% E2 (187 of 292), Japan 36% E2 (191 of 524), while the US, India and Brazil lean toward E5 (172, 38 and 34 reviews respectively).

## 7.3 Storefront notes

**United States — n = 610, mean 3.61.**
- Close to the global profile.
- **The cross-device story is most visible here:** Mac/web 4.6% and sync 3.6%. Examples: `7727790054` (support admits Mac lag), `9231182178` ($50 lifetime, refund), `13332457934`.
- The US hosts the long comparison essays behind the lifetime purchase decision (`2917668175`, `3440017816`) and the sharpest 2026 UX critique (`13665455992`).
- The CAN-SPAM complaint (`10095812056`) and the support-chat privacy report (`10638299850`) are US reviews.

**Japan — n = 524, mean 3.65.**
- **Highest payer share (16.0%), Watch complaints (5.0%) and "update made it worse" (8.2%) among large storefronts.**
- Japan was the centre of the **Jan 2018 purchase wave** (11 of 21: `2077043653`, `2093117380`, `2097705770`).
- Localisation grievances: 「たどたどしい日本語」 *"halting Japanese"* (`1565006445`), simplified-Chinese glyphs (`3253608656`), English-only support (`7596083103`, `13311766586`).
- Reordering never sticks (`4018038145` — 90 routines; `7656152222`; `13701264625`). Memo input breaks for Japanese IME (`7656211283`).
- Price rose from ¥1,200 (2018) → ¥4,800 lifetime (2019) → ¥6,000 (2021) → **¥19,000 (2026)** (`14106494461`, `14398774902` 「前に比べて値段が爆上がり」 *"the price has exploded"*).

**China — n = 296, mean 2.96 (3.77 excluding connectivity reviews).** Cluster 1 (Part 4.1).
- Design praise (10.8%) is high, and 12.2% are payers.
- The storefront is dominated by reviews saying the service cannot be reached.

**Korea — n = 292, mean 4.20.**
- Enthusiastic and largely 2019–20: simplicity 14.7% and "worth it even if expensive" (`6110426928`, `5770923833`).
- **Korea is the centre of the account-deletion failure: 16 of 47** (`5202035434`, `5436553027`, `5683409156`, `6091415990`).
- It also generates the most renewal and refund requests — refund 13 of 45 (`5213926173`, `7164301825`, `8022517166`, `8548501117`).
- Price objection 6.8%, often as a request for a discount event: 「할인하면 사겠다」 *"I'll buy if it's discounted"* (`5211451534`, `5347117394`, `6714650309`).

**United Kingdom — n = 162, mean 3.60.**
- **Highest monetisation friction among storefronts ≥100 (23.5%), cap 10.5%, subscription objection 3.1%.**
- The 2019 switch is told most sharply here: `3619346514`; `3887636788` "£5.99 → £9.99 → £24 a year".
- Also the GDPR complaint about the 2023 emails (`10101814356`).

**Russia — n = 159, mean 3.79.**
- **Highest Mac/web complaint rate (10.1%):** the Mac app was unavailable in the Russian store (`5974877379`), lost its Russian UI (`6020488457`), and did not sync (`7244646323`, `7673086187`, `9107782562`).
- "Update made it worse" 10.1%. Localisation 5.0% (`1552349488`, `2227517484`).
- Price benchmarked against Netflix (`3936231580`); no way to cancel (`8371039111`).

**Vietnam — n = 158, mean 4.61.**
- **The happiest storefront (79.7% 5★)**, but a third of reviews are generic.
- Reviewers take pride in a Vietnamese-built app ("App của người Việt", `4721534340`, `5810323969`, `14062845602`) and ask for local pricing (`4179813347`, `6229968661`, `6343893474`).

**Canada — n = 132, mean 3.68.**
- Highest simplicity praise (15.2%).
- CA$ pricing is called "highway robbery" (`6186108971`). The check-in cap hit here early (`6566048336`, `6593969693`).

**India — n = 130, mean 3.78.**
- **Payer share 17.7%.**
- Plan availability is the complaint: "only … yearly and lifetime … I would have signed up for a month" (`5583352286`).
- Students (`5071251596`, `8860733083`) and paid-but-inactive cases (`10693981551`, `10875668056`, `13550132289`).

**Germany — n = 109, mean 3.35.**
- **The most price- and subscription-averse storefront: price objection 14.7%, subscription objection 9.2%** — "Abo-Abzocke" (`3873901587`, `5336471509`), "Schon wieder ein Abo?" *"Yet another subscription?"* (`6315670170`), "in-app purchases should be banned" (`6209853497`).
- **Six of its seven email complaints come from the July 2023 wave** (`10101941744`, `10105269764`, `10105286283`, `10105314059`, `10109101853`, `10131134360`).
- The German UI string "Pisst du dabei?" (`5481722444`, `6315649053`).
- Design praise is also high (14.7%).

**Brazil — n = 105, mean 3.63.**
- **Payer share 18.1% and crash rate 9.5%.** In Oct–Nov 2019 the app would not open after purchase (`4884387541`, `4999720797`, `5129836305`, `4811572821`).
- Cap 10.5%; R$20/month (`4728517189`).
- `[limited depth: n < 150]`

**Mexico (91, 3.98), Poland (87, 3.70), Australia (87, 3.52), France (77, 3.77), Ukraine (58, 4.16), Turkey (57, 4.19), Spain (56, 3.70)** `[limited depth: under 100 each]`:
- **Spain** has the highest monetisation friction of any eligible storefront (26.8%) and 3 check-in-cap reviews (`6623842824`, `7108556777`, `7698991901`).
- **Turkey** (22.8%) and **Poland** (19.5%) are close behind. Turkey's complaints are local-currency prices: 270 TL (`4771857078`), 260 TL lifetime (`7498411514`), "under 100 TL a year" (`6966124853`). Poland's are subscription principle (`3661117378`, `3828231545`).
- **Australia** has a 25.3% 1★ share (`3879350608` "Dodge as hell"; A$40/yr `3795256803`; A$62 `5856846519`).
- **France** combines price objection (10.4%, "€40 … vous vivez où, Monaco?" *"where do you live, Monaco?"* `5122246109`), web-app slowness (`5494522026`) and translation quality (`7351181517`; fixed `11258874148`).
- **Ukraine**'s login failures (12.1%) include early registration errors (`1827275316`) and Google sign-in crashes on iPad (`6961415994`).

## 7.4 Group A — high-spend markets (defined, with disclosure)

**Definition:** `us, cn, jp, gb, de, fr, ca, au, kr` — the storefronts commonly cited as the largest App Store consumer-spend markets. **This is an external-knowledge definition, not derived from this corpus; no spend or download figure appears anywhere in this data.**

| Group | n | mean | 5★% | 1★% | cap | mon-friction | price obj. | payer | not granted | billing-int. | CN | login | Watch | Mac/web | acct-del | best | generic |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| High-spend | 2,289 (58.1%) | 3.60 | 46.5 | 20.2 | 4.9 | 12.6 | 5.2 | **12.8** | **2.4** | **4.9** | 7.6 | 5.8 | **3.2** | 3.2 | **1.7** | 6.3 | 7.5 |
| Rest of world (90 storefronts) | 1,650 (41.9%) | **3.93** | 59.3 | 14.8 | **6.1** | **14.1** | 5.2 | 8.2 | 0.8 | 2.1 | 0.0 | 1.2 | 1.6 | **4.1** | 0.5 | **9.6** | **14.6** |

**Findings:**
1. **High-spend storefronts rate lower (3.60 vs 3.93).** About half the gap is China. Without `cn`, the high-spend mean is ≈3.70, derived from the table: (3.60 × 2,289 − 2.96 × 296) / 1,993.
2. **What differs is *what money buys*.** High-spend reviewers are 1.6× as likely to be payers, 3× as likely to report Premium not granted, 2.3× as likely to report a billing-integrity problem, and 2× as likely to complain about the Watch and account deletion. **Price objection is identical (5.2% vs 5.2%).**
3. **The rest of the world reports access and affordability:** the cap (6.1% vs 4.9%) and more generic praise. **Interpretation:** in high-spend markets people pay, then hit portability and integrity problems; elsewhere, more stop at the gate.

## 7.5 Group B — high-review-volume markets (disclosed proxy)

**Definition:** the 18 storefronts with ≥50 reviews *in this corpus*. **Review volume is used only as a disclosed proxy for market presence; it is not a download or install figure.**
- **Group B:** n = 3,190 (81.0%), mean **3.71**, 5★ 50.3%, 1★ 18.5%. Payer 11.8% · not granted 2.0% · billing integrity 4.2% · China 5.4% · best 7.1% · generic 10.0%.
- **Tail (81 storefronts):** n = 749 (19.0%), mean **3.89**, 5★ 58.6%, 1★ 15.8%. Payer 6.5% · not granted 0.7% · billing integrity 1.9% · China 0.0% · best 10.3% · generic 12.4%.

**Finding:** the tail rates higher and pays less. This is consistent with Group A: the more reviewers engage with paying and with multiple devices, the more integrity and parity problems they meet.

## 7.6 Sub-50 storefronts `[limited evidence]`

81 storefronts hold 749 reviews, all included in every global figure. Observations that rise above noise:

- **Taiwan (n = 26, mean 3.27) is the lowest-rated storefront with ≥15 reviews.** Its complaints:
  - notes moved to Premium (`4166681642`);
  - lifetime bought but no count habits (`4435992248`);
  - "My data and life-time access were both deleted" (`6934749412`).
- **Netherlands (49, 3.35):** price and cap; motivational copy "far too American and over the top" (`3870589285`).
- **Italy (37, 3.65):** cap and price ("43 euro … Per una agenda???" *"For a diary???"* `5884634297`).
- **Switzerland (31, 3.84):** contains the probable duplicate "Update is worse" pair (`6428238618`, `6428288301`) and a 2023 spam complaint (`10094809568`).
- **Singapore (21, 3.76):** freezes, and support "not yet seen" for 4 days (`6118170712`); lifetime regret (`7723922183`).
- **Thailand (29, 4.28) and Malaysia (21, 4.57):** strongly positive, mainly design and simplicity.
- **Arabic-language users** ask for an Arabic UI (`6209402227` Egypt, `6623437123` Saudi Arabia, `8971467237`).

| Pattern | Where | Section |
|---|---|---|
| Cannot use the service without a VPN | cn 57.8% vs ≈0 everywhere else | 4.1 |
| Subscription-principle objection | de 9.2%, pl 4.6%, gb 3.1% vs jp 0.4%, kr/in/br/es 0% | 4.2 |
| Price objection | de 14.7%, tr 10.5%, fr 10.4%, es 8.9%, ru 8.8% vs us 3.3%, in 3.1%, cn 3.0% | 3.3 |
| Free cap | es 10.7%, gb 10.5%, br 10.5%, mx 8.8% vs cn 0%, ua 1.7%, vn 2.5% | 4.2 |
| Payer share | br 18.1%, in 17.7%, jp 16.0% vs vn 2.5%, mx 4.4% | 6 |
| Mac/web app problems | ru 10.1%, fr 7.8%, ua 6.9% vs tr/es 0% | 4.4 |
| Account-deletion failure | kr 5.5%, de 3.7% vs us 0.5% | 4.6 |
| Marketing-email complaint | de 6.4% vs global 0.9% | 4.6 |
| Localisation complaint | ru 5.0%, de 3.7%, es 3.6%, jp 3.1% | 2.4 |
| Happiest storefronts | vn 4.61, kr 4.20, tr 4.19, ua 4.16 (with high generic shares) | 7.3 |

No cultural generalisation is drawn beyond these measured differences.
