# PART 8 — TIME-TREND ANALYSIS

## 8.1 Method

- Five eras are defined from product events visible in the reviews (Part 1.6). Rates below are % of each era's reviews.
- Year figures (n, share of year) are used where an event is narrower than an era.
- Event windows (Part 1.4) isolate single releases.
- No trend is claimed from a single month.

## 8.2 Trend 1 — Sentiment dipped in 2020–23 and has largely recovered `[high-priority]`

| Series | E1 | E2 | E3 | E4 | E5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Mean (all) | 3.64 | **4.06** | **3.36** | 3.47 | 3.75 |
| Mean (substantive) | 3.79 | 3.91 | 3.24 | 3.24 | 3.60 |
| 5★ share | 46.9% | 61.7% | 41.1% | 46.5% | 52.3% |
| 1★ share | 19.6% | 12.9% | 23.6% | **24.9%** | 16.5% |

- **E1 is held down by China** (106 connectivity reviews), the v2.0 wipe and the Jan 2018 purchase wave. It is also *held up* by the Oct 2018 burst.
- **E2 is the high point:** a stable app with a lifetime option and active development.
- **E3–E4 fall** with Habitify X, the check-in cap, the Mac stall and the 2023 email wave. **2022 is the worst year** (mean 3.32, 28.8% 1★).
- **E5 recovers to 3.75** despite new redesign complaints.

## 8.3 Trend 2 — Praise moved from "simple and beautiful" to "the best, and it changed my life" `[high-priority]`

| Praise theme | E1 | E2 | E3 | E4 | E5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Core praise (union) | 42.0% | 44.6% | 25.8% | 30.7% | 32.6% |
| Simplicity | 15.9% | 15.7% | 6.0% | 6.0% | 7.3% |
| Design | 14.0% | 9.0% | 4.2% | 4.3% | 4.8% |
| "Best tracker" | 6.6% | 7.7% | 6.2% | **9.1%** | **9.2%** |
| Life outcome | 3.4% | 5.6% | 5.3% | **7.4%** | 5.4% |
| Confusing / hard to use | 1.8% | 1.8% | 3.8% | 2.4% | **5.8%** |

**Interpretation:**
- As features accumulated (goals, timer, Health, mood, challenges, friends, AI), "simple" stopped being the headline.
- Reviewers now praise capability, while "confusing" triples.
- The simplicity identity that won the app its early users is eroding.

## 8.4 Trend 3 — Money complaints track the take-backs `[high-priority]`

| Theme | E1 | E2 | E3 | E4 | E5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Monetisation friction (union) | 9.0% | **17.3%** | 14.0% | 7.2% | 14.4% |
| Free cap | 4.6% | 6.5% | 5.1% | 3.4% | 5.8% (2026: **9.3%**) |
| Price objection | 2.3% | **8.4%** | 6.0% | 2.9% | 4.1% |
| Subscription objection | 0.1% | **2.6%** | 0.6% | 0.2% | 0.1% |
| Weekly check-in cap | — | — | **4.2%** | 0.2% | 0.7% |
| Upsell surfaces | 0.4% | 0.1% | 0.2% | 0.5% | **1.7%** |
| One-time / lifetime praised | **3.0%** | 0.6% | 0.9% | 0.5% | 1.0% |
| Price praised | 1.9% | 2.5% | 2.6% | 2.4% | 3.2% |

**Each spike aligns with a dated change:**
- the subscription switch (E2: 29 subscription objections in 2019);
- the check-in cap (E3: 14 in 2020, 8 in 2021);
- the 2025–26 upsell banners and 2-habit tier (E5).

Price praise never fell — the lifetime option kept a satisfied paying base.

## 8.5 Trend 4 — Cross-device problems peaked in 2020–23 `[high-priority]`

| Theme | E1 | E2 | E3 | E4 | E5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Platform issues (union) | 3.7% | 4.7% | **18.7%** | **19.9%** | 9.7% |
| Mac / web app | 0.8% | 1.8% | 7.9% | **12.2%** | 2.2% |
| Sync | 1.4% | 1.7% | 6.2% | **7.9%** | 2.3% |
| Watch | 1.2% | 1.6% | **6.8%** | 4.6% | 1.7% |
| Widget | 0.8% | 0.4% | 4.2% | 2.2% | **5.2%** |

- The Mac fix (13.0.1) and the Watch fix (Jan 2024) show up clearly in E5.
- **Widgets are the one surface still worsening** — May 2025 redesign; 6.3% of 2025 reviews.

## 8.6 Trend 5 — Regressions come in release-shaped spikes `[very strong in E3]`

- **Regression union:** 3.8% (E1) → 2.2% (E2) → **14.9% (E3)** → 3.8% (E4) → 4.8% (E5).
- **"Update made it worse", by year:** 2016 8.8% · 2017 8.9% · 2018 0.2% · 2019 1.7% · **2020 7.6%** · 2021 6.5% · 2022 3.4% · 2023 3.8% · 2024 2.3% · **2025 5.8%** · 2026 3.3%.
- Each spike maps to one release: v2.0, the 2017 changes, Habitify X, and the 2025 notes and widget redesigns.

## 8.7 Trend 6 — Account and data trust: one bad year per failure `[meaningful]`

- **Account, email & data union:** 0.8% → 2.9% → 3.4% → **7.9%** → 1.0%.
- **Account deletion:** 23 reviews in 2020 (2.8% of the year), 10 in 2023, 1–2 a year since.
- **Marketing email:** 27 in 2023 (12.7% of that year), 1 in 2024.
- **Privacy:** 2.4% of E4.

Both failures were largely fixed after their spike, but residual cases continue (`11244516686`, `12515577285`).

## 8.8 Trend 7 — Entitlement failure improved and stayed improved `[meaningful]`

- **Billing integrity:** 5.1% → 2.7% → 5.5% → 3.4% → 2.8%.
- **Premium not granted:** **3.6% (E1)** → 0.9% → 1.9% → 1.2% → 1.2%.

The 2018 store-purchase defect did not recur at scale. What remains in E5 is **cross-platform / web-purchase portability** (`13400575929`, `13516370858`, `13732504298`) and auto-converting trials (`13422214812`, `14019544061`).

## 8.9 Trend 8 — Demand shifted from "more habit types" to "integrations and polish" `[meaningful]`

| Request / theme | E1 | E2 | E3 | E4 | E5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Multiple completions / quantities | **2.9%** | 1.8% | 0.2% | 0.2% | 0.1% |
| Scheduling | **4.1%** | 1.8% | 0.9% | 1.2% | 1.4% |
| Widget request | 0.1% | 0.1% | 1.5% | 1.2% | 1.1% |
| Health integration poor / gated | 0.1% | 0.0% | 0.8% | 1.4% | **2.2%** |
| Integrations mentioned | 0.1% | 0.6% | 1.9% | 1.9% | **2.5%** |

- Two of the largest early requests were substantially answered: multi-check-in goals (Aug 2020) and every-N-days frequency.
- Later demand concerns **Health accuracy** (sleep double-counting: `9530252692`, `10712781996`, `8460050694`), **wearables** (Garmin `13975290800`, Fitbit `14242881753`) and **interactive widgets**.

## 8.10 Trend 9 — Support: better than peers, but AI replies arrive `[meaningful]`

- **Support praised:** 2.3% → 2.0% → 4.0% → 2.9% → 3.0%.
- **Support unresponsive:** 1.8% → 1.4% → 4.2% → 2.4% → 3.1%.
- **AI features or AI support:** 8 reviews, 7 of them 2025–26, mostly negative. Examples: "Stop shoving AI everywhere" (`12324906138`); "Their support is AI" (`13659075450`); the chatbot "stops answering" (`12715605250`); an AI reply with no human (`14106494461`). **Weak signal**, but new and directional.

## 8.11 Trends explicitly NOT claimed

- **No causal claim that the App Store "Today" feature caused the China failure.** It exposed an existing failure to more users (the failure dates from 2016–18).
- **No claim that the whole Oct 2018 burst was solicited.** The burst's shape and the "Peter"/rating-email mentions are circumstantial; six reviews carry explicit solicitation signals.
- **No review-prompt trend after 2018.** Review-nag complaints are 3.8% of 2017 and ≤1% of every later year.
- **No ADHD or neurodivergent trend.** Seven reviews over ten years, all positive-to-mixed (`9696710729`, `10038284177`, `10854384133`, `14025511246`).
- **No conversion, retention or churn rate** — the review is not a funnel.
- **No monthly trend within E5.** Months range widely on small n.

---

# PART 9 — WHAT A COMPETITOR LEARNS FROM THIS CORPUS FOR FREE

1. **Time-of-day sections plus flexible frequency is a winning core.** 453 simplicity and 299 "best tracker" reviews rated 4–5★ describe it, and "no other app does [time of day] as well" (`1634301611`).
2. **A lifetime price is a moat.** It is the single most-cited purchase trigger (112 payers); rivals that only rent are named as the reason people chose Habitify (`3440017816`, `5194210441`).
3. **A lifetime price is also a promise.** Removing features from lifetime holders produces the angriest paid reviews (`6373708406`: "I wish I had got the monthly subscription so I could cancel").
4. **Cross-platform wins sales and loses stars.** Offer one account on every device only if every device ships on the same day.
5. **Never take back what a free user already has.** The check-in cap (mean 2.00) and notes-to-Premium moves generated more hostility per review than the cap itself.
6. **Make leaving easy.** Account deletion (mean 1.23) and email opt-out (mean 1.41) are the worst-rated themes in the corpus, and the cheapest to fix.
7. **China is winnable with a reachable backend.** Chinese reviewers praise the design and localisation and ask to pay (`5602217006`, `13572613236`).
8. **Guilt-based notifications backfire.** "Let's do better tomorrow, shall we?" (`8028507816`); "Yikes" (`10980648112`); "Are you sure the day is over?" (`13665455992`). Neurodivergent users say so most clearly (`13022842033`, `10854384133`).

---

# PART 10 — PRODUCT IMPLICATIONS

Every recommendation cites the findings it rests on.

## 10.1 Immediate — trust, exit and privacy (top of the list)

1. **Make account deletion work in one step, in-app and on the web, and confirm it by email.** `[4.6a, 3.2]` 47 reviews, mean 1.23; 23 in 2020 alone; still reported in 2025 (`12515577285`, `12715605250`).
2. **Put an unsubscribe link in every email and honour account deletion across marketing systems.** `[4.6b, 8.7]` 37 reviews; 27 in July 2023; GDPR and CAN-SPAM cited (`10101814356`, `10095812056`); failure still reported in May 2024 (`11244516686`).
3. **Audit the in-app help conversation list for cross-customer leakage.** `[4.6c]` `10638299850` reports other customers' messages, emails and photos visible. This is a single claim, but one that must be ruled out.
4. **Make goal-sharing opt-in and add expiring or unguessable links.** `[4.6c]` `8440977492`.
5. **Stop incentivised or solicited reviews.** `[Warning 3, 2.4]` Rating emails, contests awarding Premium, and a CEO asking for 5★ (`3367548037`, `3897029485`, `5817575296`). Apple guidelines are an external consideration, not a corpus finding.

## 10.2 Immediate — entitlement portability

6. **One entitlement per person, recognised on every platform.** `[4.3, 6.5]` Web (Paddle) purchases must unlock iOS, and iOS purchases must unlock Mac and web; merge guest and account purchases automatically. About 25 portability cases, including 6+ week resolutions (`13732504298`, `13516370858`, `6925930580`).
7. **Show the renewal date, price and a cancel link in-app for every billing channel.** `[4.3]` 13 cancellation traps (`6559520547`, `8371039111`); 45 refund reviews.
8. **Make trial conversion explicit:** a reminder before the charge, and access kept after cancelling a renewal. `[6.6]` `13422214812`, `13550132289`, `14019544061`.
9. **Never show upsell or cross-promotion to payers.** `[4.3, 4.2e]` 14 + 12 reviews (`5556783335`, `10620666635`, `12606209462`).

## 10.3 Near-term — parity, sync and reach

10. **Release parity: no feature ships on iOS without Mac, web and Watch support in the same release, or a visible "coming" state.** `[4.4]` Mac/web was 18.5% of 2022 reviews; support conceded the gap (`7727790054`).
11. **Offer iCloud sync as an option alongside the account backend**, and a reachable endpoint for mainland China. `[4.1, 4.4]` 173 China reviews; iCloud requested repeatedly (`3208341657`, `4505011830`, `9491548292`, `11626791974`).
12. **Restore interactive widgets (incremental +1, streak, compact) and fix widget refresh on iPad.** `[4.4, 8.5]` Widget complaints were 6.3% of 2025 (`12711946464`, `12832854491`, `12402275998`).
13. **Watch: single-tap completion and area / time-of-day complications back; sync without opening the phone.** `[4.4]` `12081627373`, `13224394178`, `9545911562`.
14. **Health accuracy:** de-duplicate sleep, allow manual override of Health-linked habits, and do not wipe data when switching tracking mode. `[8.9]` `10712781996`, `13517256802`.

## 10.4 Near-term — stop removing, start migrating

15. **Every removal gets a toggle or a migration.** `[4.5, 6.7]` Priorities: notes back-dating and editing (`12185327521`), the "day is over?" pop-up off switch (`13665455992`), the passcode lock (`12506599929`), and ordering that sticks (`13701264625`, `13659075450`).
16. **Keep "simple mode" defaults.** `[8.3]` Hide friends, challenges and journal until enabled; complexity complaints tripled to 5.8% of E5 (`13209298416`, `14144429082`, `9616844062`).
17. **Replace guilt copy with neutral or positive notifications, and let users choose categories.** `[9.8]` 43 reviews on unwanted or guilt notifications (`12144897668`, `13606005182`).

## 10.5 Monetisation experiments (after 10.1–10.2)

18. **Test the free tier: 3 habits + unlimited check-ins vs 5 habits vs a 14-day full trial.** `[3.3, 4.2]` The evaluation argument is the plurality; the check-in cap has the worst rating of any monetisation mechanism (2.00). Measure conversion *and* 1★ rate.
19. **Offer monthly in-app in every storefront, and regionalise lifetime pricing.** `[2.3, 7.7]` `5583352286`, `12163064741`, `14106494461` (¥19,000), Vietnam and Turkey requests.
20. **Keep the lifetime option; publish what lifetime includes.** `[6.3, 6.7]` It is the top purchase trigger.

## 10.6 Product opportunities (review-evidenced)

21. **Statistics:** an all-habit weekly grid, raw totals (not just %), and multi-year history. `[3.5]` 58 requests (`13502513407`, `13574501337`).
22. **Bad-habit counters with limits** — tap per occurrence, auto-fail over the limit. `[3.5]` `9696710729`, `12689579726`.
23. **Scheduling:** monthly nth-weekday, end dates, a vacation or pause that preserves streaks, and one-tap reschedule. `[3.5]` `10990216385`, `12352483402`, `11292910105`.
24. **Custom units, icons and per-habit colours.** `[3.5]` 56 + 48 reviews.
25. **Localisation QA for Japanese, German, French, Polish and Spanish, and local-language support replies.** `[7.3]` `3253608656`, `5481722444`, `8322104956`, `7596083103`.

## 10.7 Verification questions this report cannot answer, ranked

26. **How many accounts are stuck mid-deletion, and are they still emailed?** `[4.6]`
27. **What share of web (Paddle) purchases fail to unlock on iOS?** `[4.3]`
28. **Does the support-conversation list scope correctly by user?** `[4.6c]`
29. **Mainland-China reachability today:** is the backend still blocked? `[4.1]`
30. **Did the 2019 check-in and notes gates raise conversion enough to offset the 1★ wave?** `[4.2]` Internal data only.
31. **Were the Oct 2018 reviews tied to an email campaign?** `[Warning 3]`

## 10.8 Experiments worth running

32. **Free-tier design:** cap 3 vs 5; check-in cap vs none; trial vs none — measuring conversion, D30 retention and 1★ rate. `[10.5]`
33. **Simple vs full default UI for new users.** `[10.4]`
34. **Interactive-widget variant vs current.** `[10.3]`
35. **Notification tone:** neutral vs current, among users who self-identify as neurodivergent or who opt in. `[10.4]`

---

# PART 11 — EVIDENCE APPENDIX

## 11.1 Counting rules

- **Denominators.**
  - Every global percentage uses **3,939** (all records, no removals). The substantive series, where stated, uses **2,336** (body > 60 characters).
  - Era denominators: E1 = 929, E2 = 1,252, E3 = 530, E4 = 417, E5 = 811. Year denominators: 2016 136 · 2017 157 · 2018 571 · 2019 722 · 2020 815 · 2021 310 · 2022 205 · 2023 212 · 2024 219 · 2025 378 · 2026 214.
  - Storefront denominators are that storefront's own record count.
  - Segment denominators are always labelled as such: explicit payers 427; 5★ 2,044; 4★ 526; 3★ 383; 2★ 279; 1★ 707.
- **Themes are non-exclusive.** Every review received ≥1 code (mean 2.1 per review).
- **Unions** are computed on distinct review IDs from their components:
  - `U_MON_FRICTION` = CAP PAYWALL PRICE- AFFORD SUB- UPSELL DISCLOSE CHECKLIMIT PRICEHIKE MONTHLYREQ ADS ONETIMEREQ.
  - `U_BILLING_INTEGRITY` = ENT BILL REFUND CANCEL BUYFAIL ADSPAID LEGACY PROMOFAIL.
  - `U_RELIABILITY` = CRASH FREEZE DATALOSS BUG SYNC LOGIN NOTIF- WIDGET- WATCH- HEALTH- XPLAT- STATS- TZ BADGE.
  - `U_ACCOUNT_DATA` = ACCTDEL EMAIL PRIVACY ACCOUNT.
  - `U_REGRESSION` = UPDATE- REMOVED.
  - `U_CORE_PRAISE` = USEFUL SIMPLE DESIGN MOTIV OUTCOME STATS+ BEST.
  - `U_UNMET` = all `R_*` codes + ONETIMEREQ + MONTHLYREQ.
  - `U_PLATFORM_ISSUE` = XPLAT- WATCH- WIDGET- SYNC.
  - `U_SUPPORT_FAIL` = SUPPORT- ACCTDEL.
  - `U_GENERIC_ONLY` = reviews whose only codes are GEN+/GEN-/GEN0/JUNK/MISRATE.
- **"Payer"** requires a first-person statement of payment, subscription or lifetime ownership in any language. Conditional statements ("I'd buy if…") are coded `BUYIF`, not `PAYER`.
- **`CAP` vs `CAPOK`.** `CAP` is the habit limit expressed as friction; `CAPOK` is the limit accepted or defended. Reviews using the cap as their reason to buy are coded `PAYER` without `CAP`.
- **`CNNET`** requires a mainland-China network, VPN or server reference, or is a registration/guide-stuck failure from `cn` in the 2018–19 waves. A vague "can't use" is coded `CRASH` only.
- **Rounding.** Percentages are rounded to 2 dp globally and 1 dp in storefront and era tables; means to 2 dp.
- **Qualitative splits** (Part 3.3) are labelled as such and are **not** additional quantified themes.

## 11.2 Theme definitions (codebook)

Each code is applied by the analyst on reading the full review. The labels in 11.6 give each code's one-line definition. The rules that needed judgement:

| Code | Rule |
|---|---|
| `GEN+` / `GEN-` / `GEN0` | Affective content only, no specific theme ("Good", "좋아요", "Tốt", "Great app") |
| `USEFUL` | States the app helps them build/track habits, with no more specific praise |
| `OUTCOME` | A concrete change in the reviewer's life (a habit acquired, weight, books read, depression eased, a quit). "Helps me" alone is `USEFUL` |
| `BEST` / `COMP` | `BEST`: says it is the best tracker (they have used). `COMP`: names a competitor or says they tried several |
| `STATS+` / `STATS-` | Praise vs complaint about statistics, streak or completion-rate *display or calculation* |
| `CAP` / `CHECKLIMIT` / `PAYWALL` | Habit-count limit / weekly check-in limit / any other gated feature or "have to pay" |
| `SUB-` vs `PRICE-` | `SUB-`: objects to subscriptions as a model. `PRICE-`: objects to the level ("too expensive") |
| `ENT` | Paid (or says they paid) and Premium is not active, was lost, or cannot be restored. `ENTFIX` is added when the review reports resolution |
| `BILL` | Charged unexpectedly, overcharged, or uses scam/fraud language about money |
| `CANCEL` | Cannot find a way to cancel, or charged after cancelling or deleting |
| `LOGIN` | Cannot register, sign in or stay signed in (any platform). `CNNET` adds the China-network reason |
| `XPLAT-` | Mac, web, iPad, Android or Windows client broken, missing features, not syncing or unavailable |
| `SYNC` | Data does not sync between devices, or sync is missing (incl. iCloud requests) |
| `UPDATE-` / `REMOVED` | An update is blamed for making things worse / a named feature no longer exists |
| `ACCTDEL` | Account deletion fails or is impossible |
| `EMAIL` | Unwanted marketing / progress emails, unsubscribe not working |
| `NOTIFNAG` vs `NOTIF-` | Too many, guilt-inducing or un-disableable notifications vs reminders not arriving or wrong |
| `R_*` | A request for a capability that did not exist *for that reviewer at that time* |
| `MISRATE` | The star rating clearly contradicts the text (e.g. 5★ "can't open") |
| `SOLICIT` | The review names the founder in a prompted way, mentions a rating email or incentive, or reports a request to review |
| `JUNK` | Test messages, gibberish or off-topic text |

The full codebook (110 codes) is in the working file `codes/codebook.md` (reproduction, 11.5). Every code used appears with its label and complete ID list in 11.6.

## 11.3 Manual corrections applied (full list)

**During reading** — a code introduced mid-corpus and back-applied to earlier batches:
- `R_COUNT` (multiple completions per day) was split out of `R_SCHED` / `R_CUSTOM` after batch 4 and back-applied to `1516266035`, `1745724575`, `1751385257`, `1833153877`, `2040190339`, `2060287556`, `2069641389`, `2108125280`.
- `MISRATE` was added to `2096241882`; `UPDATE+` to `2132082291`.

**Coverage check:** two reviews in batch 1 had been skipped and were coded — `1548983585` (PAYER R_CUSTOM) and `1556318326` (PAYWALL USEFUL).

**Recall sweep** — all candidates read; 33 corrections:
- `+REFUND`: `9751191776`, `14040060412` (also `+PAYER`).
- `+HEALTH+`: `6496710391`.
- `+HEALTH-`: `7265387980`, `7765702076`.
- `+R_HEALTH`: `13209298416`.
- `+AI` (chatbot support): `10729873620`, `12715605250`.
- `+EMAIL`: `3559365998`.
- `+SYNC`: `6523205254`, `4342286453`, `5338564855`, `6359290822`, `8417350024`, `7112692402`, `7847153677`, `9241253490`.
- `+WATCH+`: `3375623283`, `3389713889`, `4125870536`, `6051790562`, `6137145408`, `6341023589`, `2086169314`, `3295388458`, `6481448783`, `1987277264`, `3678466422`.
- `+R_WIDGET`: `3294101975`, `8962820804`.
- `+PRICE-`: `7723922183`, `14023238746`.
- `+SUB-`: `10131134360`.

**Recall candidates reviewed and rejected (no change):**
- 126 "ADHD" matches (the verb "add");
- ~150 "subscription" mentions in praise or neutral context;
- ~80 "sync" mentions that praise sync;
- CAP / CHECKLIMIT / ENT / DATALOSS / CANCEL candidates already carrying the correct neighbouring code.

## 11.4 Validation approach and known error risk

- **Control:** all 3,939 records were read in full, in date order, and coded at the time of reading. The codes file is the audit trail, and every review's codes are listed in 11.7.
- **Structural checks:** every ID coded exactly once; every code valid; union and segment counts recomputed from codes.
- **Recall checks:** 20 multilingual keyword patterns across 21 themes; every untagged candidate was read (Part 11.3).
- **Direction of residual error:**
  - *Under-coding* is most likely for secondary themes in long multi-issue reviews (a fifth issue mentioned in passing).
  - *Over-coding* risk is highest for `USEFUL` / `SIMPLE` in short non-English praise, and for `CNNET` on vague 2018 China "can't use" reviews (a conservative rule was applied, 11.1).
  - Borderline payer calls ("I'll buy", "paid version is worth it") were resolved toward `BUYIF` unless payment was explicit.
- **Not validated by a second rater.**

## 11.5 External sources and reproduction

| Claim | Source | Status |
|---|---|---|
| Membership of "high-spend markets" (Part 7.4) | External general knowledge of the largest App Store consumer-spend storefronts | **Not derived from this corpus. No spend or download figure appears in this data.** Disclosed as an assumption |
| "High-review-volume markets" (Part 7.5) | This corpus's own review counts | Disclosed proxy. **Not downloads** |
| Developer "Unstatic Ltd Co", bundle `co.vuvo.habitify`, app ID 1111447047 | `manifest.json` | Corpus metadata |
| Everything else | The 3,939 reviews | Direct |

**No product website, App Store listing, press material or third-party data was consulted.** Where reviewers contradict each other (the free cap being 2 or 3; whether Health sync is free), the contradiction is reported rather than resolved.

```
cd "app store review/33. Habitify - Habit Tracker - Daily Goals, Routine & Streaks"
# 1. prep.py    — reconcile: records == unique ids == sum(by_country) == manifest.total == 3,939;
#                 per-country (99) and rating distributions == manifest; emit 19 readable batches
#                 (date order, double HTML-unescaped, lines > 1,500 chars wrapped, never truncated)
# 2. read every batch in full; hand-code each review -> codes/c01..c19.txt (one line per review_id)
# 3. load.py    — verify every review_id coded exactly once with valid codes; apply codes/patches.txt
# 4. recall.py  — 20 multilingual keyword patterns; list untagged candidates; read all; patch misses
# 5. stats.py / extra.py — aggregates by theme, star band, year, era, storefront, market group,
#                 payer segment and event windows; ids.json; codes_final.json
# 6. appendix.py — Parts 11.6 and 11.7 generated from ids.json / codes_final.json
```

**Corpus-level facts any reproduction must match:**
- 3,939 records · 99 storefronts · 0 duplicate IDs · 0 empty bodies;
- 16 duplicate-text groups (50 records, retained) · 169 `is_edited` records;
- rating distribution `{5:2044, 4:526, 3:383, 2:279, 1:707}` · mean 3.7416;
- substantive subset n = 2,336, mean 3.63 · reviews ≤25 characters n = 820, mean 4.04;
- date range 2016-08-03 → 2026-09-02.
